In real-world systems, couriers are not exclusive to a single platform and may concurrently serve multiple platforms, while each platform can only observe its own orders and couriers' interactions due to privacy and operational constraints. This results in a multi-platform dispatch environment with inherent partial observability. However, most existing works on dispatch optimization assume full courier observability and mandatory assignment acceptance, causing substantial performance degradation when deployed in realistic multi-platform settings. In this paper, we propose POLO, a partially observable multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for dispatching optimization in multi-platform instant delivery systems. POLO firstly models each platform-grid pair as an independent agent that learns dispatch policies solely from platform-local observations, aligning the learning process with real-world privacy and operational constraints. To support effective decision-making under incomplete and heterogeneous courier information, POLO introduces a novel attention-based policy representation that selectively aggregates inter-courier information. Moreover, we design a counterfactual reward shaping mechanism to mitigate the non-stationarity induced by joint actions across grids, leading to more stable and scalable learning. We develop a high-fidelity simulator to evaluate dispatch performance under varying numbers of platforms and system scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate that POLO consistently outperforms strong baselines in terms of platform revenue and courier travel efficiency, highlighting its robustness and effectiveness in realistic multi-platform settings.

</details>


### [67] [ReOrder-OPD:Reliability-Aware Prompt Ordering for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10905)
*Ximo Zhu,Ruiqi Liu,Rong Wang,Ping Wu,Xiang Zheng,Wenzhuo Xu,Xubin Yao,Zhiyuan Yan,Bo Li,Jun Gao,Xiaolei Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReOrder-OPD方法，通过定义提示级别教师续写可靠性R，并利用最大ROUGE-5 F1作为代理指标对提示进行排序，再对高R提示优先进行on-policy蒸馏训练，在数学和代码设置中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）中教师监督并非总是可靠，现有方法依赖局部置信度或师生一致性来加权、过滤或截断轨迹，这些信号无法直接判断教师能否从学生前缀继续到正确答案；轨迹级别的干预还会混淆单次rollout的不可靠性与该提示的低期望训练价值。

Method: 定义提示级别的教师续写可靠性R（教师从学生前缀到达正确答案的概率，对当前学生诱导的前缀和轨迹求平均）。由于估计R需要大量教师续写，采用一个独立学生轨迹与验证器正确同提示教师轨迹之间的最大ROUGE-5 F1作为代理指标。ReOrder-OPD按代理分数对提示排序，然后为朴素OPD抽取独立的on-policy训练轨迹进行蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen3和Gemma4数学设置及Qwen3代码设置中，ReOrder-OPD优于所有对应聚合比较；在全部六个FiRe-OPD和ExOPD设置中均获得性能提升。代理分数与真实R的均值在十个等频分箱中呈现单调上升关系，验证了代理能有效区分粗粒度可靠性水平。

Conclusion: 提示顺序排序与轨迹内监督是互补的，ReOrder-OPD通过代理指标对提示按可靠性排序，能有效提升OPD训练效果，且可与其他轨迹级监督方法结合使用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) applies token-level teacher supervision to student-generated trajectories, but this supervision is not always reliable. Existing methods use local confidence or teacher-student agreement to weight, filter, or truncate the sampled trajectory. These signals do not directly determine whether the teacher can continue a student prefix to a correct answer, and trajectory-level interventions can conflate one rollout's unreliability with low expected training value of its prompt. We define prompt-level teacher continuation reliability $R$ as the teacher's probability of reaching a correct answer from a student prefix, averaged over prefixes and trajectories induced by the current student. Oracle experiments show that high-$R$ prompts yield larger OPD gains and that descending-$R$ training outperforms random and ascending orders on a fixed prompt pool. Because estimating $R$ requires many teacher continuations, we use the maximum ROUGE-5 F1 between one independent student rollout and verifier-correct same-prompt teacher trajectories. Across ten equal-frequency bins of this actual score, mean $R$ rises monotonically, showing that the proxy separates coarse reliability levels. ReOrder-OPD sorts prompts by the proxy, then draws independent on-policy training trajectories for vanilla OPD. It improves every matched aggregate comparison across Qwen3 and Gemma4 mathematics settings and Qwen3 code settings. Gains in all six FiRe-OPD and ExOPD settings show that prompt ordering complements within-trajectory supervision.

</details>


### [68] [Physics-informed Diffusion Generative Model for Time-Series Data Synthesis in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10941)
*Haiteng Wang,Yunfei Zhu,Tao Wang,Yikang Li,Jiabao Dong,Xiaoge Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysDGM，一种逐步嵌入物理规律的扩散生成模型，用于合成与动力学系统物理规律一致的时间序列数据。该方法在反向扩散的每一步都嵌入物理约束，确保轨迹级别的物理一致性。基于该方法构建的440万样本合成数据集在34个数据集上表现出优异的保真度，下游任务性能相比仅用真实数据提升了15%-48%，且训练数据需求减少10-20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时间序列信号（如涡轮温度和转速）对监测复杂动力学系统的健康状态至关重要，但受限于恶劣环境和高昂的实验测试成本，数据采集困难。现有方法缺乏在生成过程中嵌入物理规律的能力，无法保证生成的时序数据与系统底层物理规律一致。

Method: PhysDGM将物理规律直接嵌入到反向扩散生成过程的每一个步骤中，而非仅在最终输出时施加约束，从而确保生成的时间序列在轨迹层面上满足物理一致性。该方法通过逐步的物理约束引导生成过程，构建了大规模的AI合成数据集。

Result: 在34个数据集（涵盖涡扇发动机、航空发动机、电池和化工过程）上验证了高保真度。加入合成数据后，剩余使用寿命预测提升48%，健康指标估计提升15%，健康状态评估提升22%，故障诊断提升20%。训练数据需求比现有方法减少10-20倍，并在航空发动机早期故障识别中展示出潜力。

Conclusion: PhysDGM为生成物理一致的工业时间序列提供了坚实基础，为物理引导AI在数据稀缺环境（包括工业机械和复杂化学反应动力学）中的广泛应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Industrial time-series signals, such as turbine temperature and rotational speed in aero-engines, are essential for monitoring the health and operational status of complex dynamical systems. However, collecting such data is often limited by harsh environments (e.g., high temperature and high pressure) and the high cost of experimental testing. To address this challenge, we introduce PhysDGM, a stepwise physics-embedded diffusion generative model for synthesizing time-series data that are consistent with the underlying physical laws of dynamical systems. PhysDGM embeds physical laws directly into each reverse diffusion step of the generative process, ensuring trajectory-level physical consistency, rather than enforcing constraints only at the final output. A large-scale AI-synthetic dataset (4.4 million samples, 20x scale-up) constructed by PhysDGM demonstrates strong fidelity across 34 datasets spanning turbofan engines, aero-engines, batteries, and chemical processes. After incorporating the synthetic data, the downstream task performance substantially surpassed that using real data alone by 48% for remaining useful life prediction, 15% for health indicator estimation, 22% for state-of-health assessment, and 20% for fault diagnosis. Moreover, it requires 10-20x less training data than existing approaches, substantially reducing the high cost of data collection in dynamical systems. We further demonstrate PhysDGM's potential in identifying early-stage faults in aero-engines by incorporating AI-synthesized data. In summary, PhysDGM provides a solid foundation for generating physically consistent industrial time-series, paving the way for expanding physics-guided AI into diverse data-scarce environments, including both industrial machinery and complex chemical reaction dynamics.

</details>


### [69] [GARLIC: Graph Attention-based Relational Learning of Multivariate Time Series in Intensive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10969)
*Ruirui Wang,Yanke Li,Manuel Günther,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GARLIC模型，一种用于不规则采样且存在大量缺失值的ICU多变量时间序列预测的图注意力神经网络架构，通过指数衰减编码器进行数据填补、时间滞后摘要图捕获传感器间依赖关系，并利用交替解耦优化方案实现准确且可解释的临床结局预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU医疗数据通常是不规则采样且严重缺失的异质多变量时间序列，但临床应用需要既准确又可解释的预测模型。现有方法在准确性和可解释性之间难以兼顾。

Method: GARLIC模型包含三个核心模块：(1)基于可学习指数衰减编码器的数据填补机制；(2)通过时间滞后摘要图捕获传感器间依赖关系；(3)融合全局模式与跨维度序列注意力机制。所有注意力权重和图边均端到端学习，提供观测层、信号层和边层的内置解释。同时设计了交替解耦优化方案来协调辅助重构与主分类目标，稳定训练过程。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、PhysioNet 2019和MIMIC-III三个ICU基准数据集上取得新的最优结果，显著提升AUROC和AUPRC，且计算成本与最优基线相当。消融实验验证了各模块的贡献，特征移除实验验证了归因保真度（完整特征>前50%>随机50%>后50%的单调性能下降）。在ICU之外的多种时间序列数据集上也展示了优越性。

Conclusion: GARLIC标志着朝不规则采样ICU时间序列数据的准确且可解释的深度学习迈出了重要一步，具备实时风险预警和透明解释的能力，并展现出跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Healthcare data, such as Intensive Care Unit (ICU) records, comprise heterogeneous multivariate time series sampled at irregular intervals with pervasive missingness. However, clinical applications demand predictive models that are both accurate and interpretable. We present our Graph Attention-based Relational Learning for Intensive Care (GARLIC) model, a novel neural network architecture that imputes missing data through a learnable exponential-decay encoder, captures inter-sensor dependencies via time-lagged summary graphs, and fuses global patterns with cross-dimensional sequential attention. All attention weights and graph edges are learned end-to-end to serve as built-in observation-, signal-, and edge-level explanations. To reconcile auxiliary reconstruction and primary classification objectives, we developed an alternating decoupled optimization scheme that stabilizes training. On three ICU benchmarks (PhysioNet 2012 & 2019, MIMIC-III), GARLIC sets the new state of the art in outcome prediction, significantly improving AUROC and AUPRC over best-performing baselines at comparable computational cost. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of each module, and feature-removal trials validate the fidelity of importance attribution through a monotonic performance drop (full > top 50% > random 50% > bottom 50%). Real-time case studies demonstrate actionable risk warnings with transparent explanations, marking a significant advance toward accurate, explainable deep learning for irregularly sampled ICU time series data. Moreover, we demonstrated \proposed{}'s superiority in data imputation and classification on various time-series datasets beyond the ICU domain, showing its generalizability and applicability to broader tasks.

</details>


### [70] [DEFT: Data-Efficient Frequency-domain Top-k Sampling via Inverse Discrete Fourier Transform for Spatiotemporal Dynamical Systems Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11019)
*Hengbo Xiao,Jiale Liu,Jiahao Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFT，一种频域数据采样方法，通过识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模式并系统性地变化其振幅和相位，利用逆离散傅里叶变换生成物理一致性的训练数据，用于偏微分方程（PDE）驱动的时空动态系统的算子学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建模PDE驱动的时空动态系统面临两大挑战：物理仿真器计算成本高昂，而纯数据驱动模型需要大量训练数据且泛化能力差，难以适应下游动态运行条件。

Method: DEFT方法在频域中识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模式，系统性地扰动对应模式的振幅和相位，通过逆离散傅里叶变换生成物理一致性的训练数据。同时推导了该方法的泛化界，并提供了理论上选择主导模式数量K的准则。

Result: 三组实验验证：1）在经典PDE求解上优于传统方法；2）在PDEBench的扩散-吸附和Burgers方程上减少40%数据需求，仅损失不到2%的预测精度；3）在电池退化PDE系统中R²超过0.99，且频域特征可迁移至其他电池化学体系，仅需20%微调数据。

Conclusion: DEFT是一种高效的数据采样方法，能够降低算子学习的数据需求并提升泛化能力，尤其在系统由少数显著频率分量主导的场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Modeling spatiotemporal dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) poses two major challenges: it either requires expensive physics-based simulators that entail iterative numerical solving at high computational cost, or it depends on abundant training data, yet purely data-driven models often generalize poorly to downstream dynamic operating conditions. We propose DEFT, a frequency-domain data sampling method that identifies the dominant Fourier modes of a physical system and systematically varies the corresponding amplitudes and phases to generate physically consistent training data via the inverse discrete Fourier transform. In addition, we derive a generalization bound of this method. We note that it also provides a theoretically principled criterion for selecting $K$. We evaluate the proposed method through three sets of experiments, each targeting a distinct aspect of its utility. First, we validate the framework on canonical PDEs solving demonstrating that it outperforms traditional methods when the system is dominated by a few prominent frequency components. Second, we employ DEFT as a data-value filter on the diffusion--sorption and Burgers equations of PDEBench, showing that it reduces data requirements by $40\%$ while sacrificing less than $2\%$ in predictive accuracy. Third, to evaluate DEFT for more challenging and practically relevant problems, we validate it in the battery degradation PDE system, achieving consistently high predictive accuracy across various test datasets with $R^2$ values exceeding $0.99$. Moreover, the learned frequency-domain features transfer to other battery chemistries with only $20\%$ of the fine-tuning data. These results demonstrate that DEFT is an effective data-sampling method for efficient operator learning.

</details>


### [71] [Derivative Computation in PINNs: Automatic Differentiation, Finite Differences and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11020)
*Maciej J. Mikulski,Tadeusz Uhl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在物理信息神经网络（PINNs）中使用有限差分（FD）替代自动微分（AD）来计算导数的方法。研究发现，在三个基准偏微分方程上，通过适当校准步长，FD在精度上与AD相当，但在整个批次大小范围内运行更快且使用更少的GPU内存。作者还提出了一种随机变体，在平稳问题上优于AD。此外，对于具有样本间依赖的神经网络架构（如BatchNorm、自注意力），标准PyTorch自动微分用法存在静默错误，而正确的逐样本替代方案在PINN相关批次大小下计算不可行，FD则提供了一种前向近似，经验上比真实逐样本导数接近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs通常依赖自动微分（AD）计算损失函数中的导数项，但AD存在高内存消耗和计算开销的问题。作者旨在探索有限差分（FD）作为替代方案的可行性，尤其是在标准AD在具有样本间依赖的架构中失效的情况下。

Method: 作者在三个基准PDE上系统比较了FD与AD在PINNs中的表现，校准FD步长以确保精度，并提出了一种随机FD变体。同时，研究还分析了在含BatchNorm和自注意力等样本间依赖架构下，标准PyTorch autograd的逐样本导数计算错误，并验证了FD作为前向近似的有效性。

Result: 在三个基准PDE上，校准后的FD在精度上与AD匹配，同时在全部测试批次范围内运行更快、GPU内存占用更少。提出的随机FD变体在平稳问题上优于AD。对于样本间依赖架构，标准autograd是静默错误的，而FD近似比真实逐样本导数误差低一个数量级。

Conclusion: FD可作为PINNs中AD的高效替代方案，在精度不损失的前提下显著提升计算效率和内存利用率，并且对具有样本间依赖的架构提供了更可靠的导数近似。

Abstract: We systematically investigate finite-difference (FD) derivative computation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) as an alternative to automatic differentiation (AD). On three benchmark PDEs we show that, with a properly calibrated step size, FD matches AD in accuracy on every problem while running faster across the full tested batch-size range and using substantially less GPU memory, and that a stochastic variant we propose outperforms AD on a stationary problem. We further show that for neural architectures with inter-sample dependencies (e.g. BatchNorm, self-attention) the standard PyTorch autograd idiom is silently incorrect; the correct per-sample alternative is computationally infeasible at PINN-relevant batch sizes, while FD provides a forward-only approximation that is empirically an order of magnitude closer to the true per-sample derivative.

</details>


### [72] [Mapping and Measuring the Behavioral Evolution of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11027)
*Dong Qiao,Chris Ding,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标注的模型行为分析框架，利用10,000个共享提示下32个语言模型（来自6个模型家族）的响应嵌入，构建三种互补的句子级不相似性度量（对齐平均每提示距离、PCA压缩的逐提示分歧摘要、无对齐的Gromov-Wasserstein差异），用于研究模型家族的组织结构和跨代际的时间演变。研究发现模型家族形成一致聚类、跨家族距离随时间递减、近期推理模型响应云更紧凑，且该框架对编码器选择具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准排行榜仅能概括模型的性能表现，却无法揭示模型之间的行为关系以及跨代际的行为变化。作者希望超越简单的性能排名，深入理解不同模型家族的行为组织方式及其随时间的演变规律。

Method: 作者收集32个模型（6个家族）对10,000个共享提示的响应，对每个响应嵌入后构建三种互补的句子级不相似性度量：(1) 对齐的平均每提示距离（伪度量）；(2) PCA压缩的逐提示分歧摘要；(3) 无对齐的Gromov-Wasserstein差异，比较模型内部响应几何。通过行为地图、家族漂移、层次聚类、跨家族收敛和响应云离散度等工具，在发布日期轴上分析静态组织和时间变化。同时从测度论视角明确对齐与不变性假设，并建立行为相似性与推理提示覆盖、小额外群体对数损失及相似有效目标分布之间的充分条件，最后用多种编码器（最小缩小73倍）验证鲁棒性。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 六个模型家族在三种构建下均形成一致的聚类，GPT-2是全局异常值；(2) 跨家族距离随发布时间递减，表明模型行为趋于收敛；(3) 近期多个推理导向模型具有相对紧凑的响应云。基于token级每提示最大均值差异（MMD）的交叉验证与句子级平均距离高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.98），并复现相同定性发现。三种额外编码器（最小为原模型的1/73）均保持排名几何、异常值和时间趋势符号。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个标签无关、编码器鲁棒的模型行为分析管道，通过测度论视角使对齐与不变性假设透明化，并揭示了模型家族聚类组织、跨代际行为收敛等趋势。所建立的充分条件为行为相似性提供了训练侧的理论解释框架，但作者强调这是可能的理论刻画而非经验验证。

Abstract: Benchmark leaderboards summarize how well a language model performs, but not how its behavior relates to that of other models or changes across generations. We characterize the output behavior of 32 models from six families using their responses to a shared bank of 10{,}000 prompts. After embedding each response, we construct three complementary sentence-level dissimilarities: an aligned mean per-prompt distance, which is a pseudometric on observed model responses; a PCA-compressed summary of prompt-wise disagreement; and an alignment-free Gromov--Wasserstein discrepancy between models' internal response geometries. We use these constructions to study static organization and temporal change on a release-date axis through behavioral maps, family-wise drift, hierarchical clustering, cross-family convergence, and response-cloud dispersion. Across the three constructions, model families form coherent clusters, with \texttt{gpt-2} as a global outlier; cross-family distances decrease over time; and several recent reasoning-oriented models have comparatively compact response clouds. A token-level cross-check based on per-prompt Maximum Mean Discrepancy closely agrees with the sentence-level mean distance (Spearman $ρ=0.98$) and recovers the same qualitative findings. We organize these comparisons through a measure-theoretic lens making their alignment and invariance assumptions explicit. We also establish an architecture-agnostic sufficient condition linking behavioral similarity to inference-prompt coverage, small excess population log-loss, and similar effective target distributions---a possible training-side account rather than an empirical explanation of the observed trends. Our pipeline is label-free, and re-encoding every response with three further encoders---down to one $73\times$ smaller---preserves the rank geometry, the outliers, and the sign of the time trend.

</details>


### [73] [ReRound: Reconstructive Rounding to Resolve Midpoint Ambiguity in Calibration-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11045)
*He-Yen Hsieh,H. T. Kung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRound是一种后训练量化方法，通过训练条件扩散模型生成低比特权重的连续重建，作为引导信号来解决RTN量化在中点附近的歧义问题，适用于小规模LLM的3-bit和4-bit权重量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RTN（四舍五入到最近）量化方案在处理接近量化区间中点（即两个量化整数正中间）的权重时存在方向模糊性，这种歧义会影响量化精度，尤其在小规模LLM上更为明显。

Method: ReRound首先训练一个条件扩散模型来生成低比特权重的连续重建信号，用于消除中点附近权重取整方向的歧义。引入容差（tolerance）指标衡量量化后权重（非最终量化整数）距中点的距离：位于容差区间内的权重使用扩散重建引导量化，而靠近量化边界的权重仍使用RTN。通过扫描容差参数生成多个候选量化整数矩阵，选择去量化后矩阵的前导奇异值与原始全精度权重最匹配的候选，以此确定最终容差参数。

Result: 在多种小规模LLM上，ReRound在3-bit和4-bit权重量化中持续优于标准RTN，同时也优于大量无校准方法，可与依赖校准的方法相竞争。整个方法完全离线运行，低比特推理阶段无额外开销。

Conclusion: ReRound代表着一种新的低比特量化方法，其应用范围不限于LLM，可推广至其他AI模型，本文聚焦于其在小规模LLM上的应用。

Abstract: ReRound (Reconstructive Rounding) is a post-training quantization method that addresses the midpoint ambiguity inherent in standard round-to-nearest (RTN) schemes when quantizing weights near the centers of quantization intervals.
  Starting from a pretrained LLM, ReRound trains a conditional diffusion model to produce continuous reconstructions of low-bit weights for the LLM. These reconstructed weights act as a guidance signal to disambiguate the rounding direction of weights located close to interval midpoints.
  To integrate this reconstruction-guided rounding with conventional RTN, ReRound introduces a tolerance metric measuring how far the quantized weight (not the final quantized integer) is away from the midpoint: quantized weights within a tolerance region around midpoints are quantized using diffusion-based reconstructions, whereas weights closer to quantization boundaries are quantized with RTN. By sweeping the tolerance parameter, ReRound generates multiple candidate quantized integer weight matrices and selects the de-quantized weight matrix candidate whose leading singular values most closely match those of the original full-precision weights. This selected candidate determines the tolerance parameter ReRound uses.
  ReRound is particularly effective for smaller LLMs. Across a range of such models, it consistently outperforms standard RTN for 3-bit and 4-bit weight quantization. ReRound achieves superior accuracy compared to an extensive set of calibration-free methods, remains competitive with calibration-dependent approaches, and operates entirely offline, introducing no additional overhead during low-bit inference.
  The ReRound strategy represents a new approach for low-bit quantization. The method applies to AI models beyond LLMs. This paper focuses on its applications to small LLMs.

</details>


### [74] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息矩阵结构的双层级逆向强化学习方法，利用Fisher矩阵与内层目标Hessian的关系推导出结构化超梯度，并通过流式频谱草图近似逆Fisher-向量积，从而避免显式构造大型Fisher矩阵，在离散和连续控制环境中取得了有竞争力的策略性能和奖励排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向强化学习的双层级公式化在外层更新时需要计算涉及内层目标逆Hessian-向量积的超梯度，这在计算上非常困难，成为了实际应用的瓶颈。

Method: 首先证明在内层最优解处，内层目标函数的Hessian与策略的Fisher信息矩阵成比例，从而得到与自然超梯度下降密切相关的结构化Fisher超梯度；然后使用流式频谱草图近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构造大型Fisher矩阵，降低曲率存储复杂度。

Result: 在离散和连续控制环境中，与一阶随机双层级基线相比，该方法取得了有竞争力的策略性能和强奖励排序质量；Fisher草图相比显式Fisher求解器降低了曲率存储复杂度并提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过Fisher信息矩阵的结构性洞察和频谱草图近似，有效解决了双层级IRL中超梯度计算的可扩展性问题，在保持性能的同时显著降低了计算和存储开销。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [75] [Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning for Genomics Applications: Insights from an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11054)
*Sepideh Saran,Mahsa Ghanbari,Uwe Ohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度学习方法在基因组学中的不确定性量化（UQ）进行了系统性实证分析，对比了深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和MC-dropout三种方法，评估了它们在不同基因组学场景下的可靠性，并提出了适用性指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型已成为基因组学应用的标准计算工具，但不确定性量化在该领域很少受到系统性的关注，其不同不确定性估计方法的可靠性缺乏评估。

Method: 在序列-活性模型和单细胞表达分析两个基因组学应用领域中进行一系列实验，系统对比深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和MC-dropout方法，评估它们在类不平衡和分布外数据等不同场景下的不确定性量化能力。

Result: 贝叶斯神经网络尽管计算成本较高，但在捕捉由强类不平衡和分布外数据引起的不确定性方面表现更优；不确定性分数可用于筛选蛋白质-RNA相互作用中的高质量预测。

Conclusion: 提供了一个系统性的比较框架，为基因组学中UQ方法的适用性和可靠性提供了指导原则，阐明了不同方法在不同场景下的优势和局限性。

Abstract: Deep learning models have emerged as the standard computational tool for a wide range of applications in genomics. Yet, uncertainty quantification (UQ) -- and more specifically, the reliability of different uncertainty estimates in this domain -- has received little systematic attention. This work presents an empirical analysis of UQ in deep learning models, focusing on genomics applications. In a series of experiments, we contrast Deep Ensembles, Bayesian Neural Networks, and Monte Carlo-dropout methods. We assess their ability to quantify uncertainty in different scenarios, accounting for common dataset characteristics in two genomic application areas and modalities: sequence-to-activity models, and single-cell expression analysis. Our systematic comparison framework provides guidelines for the applicability and reliability of UQ methods in genomics, highlighting their strengths and limitations in different scenarios. We show that Bayesian Neural Networks are better at capturing uncertainty caused by strong class imbalance and out-of-distribution data in genomics, despite their computational disadvantages. Moreover, we show how uncertainty scores can be used to select high-quality predictions in protein-RNA interactions.

</details>


### [76] [Batch Size or Negatives? A Selection Rule for Memory-Constrained Recommender Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11061)
*Artyom Sabitov,Daniil Volkov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定内存预算下，采样softmax训练推荐系统时如何分配批量大小n和负样本数k的问题，理论上证明n尽量大、k尽量小（k~1）能获得最快的收敛速度，并通过实验验证了这一配置能带来更快收敛和更好的推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经推荐系统通常使用softmax交叉熵目标进行训练，而最终分类层需要O(nK)的内存开销。采样softmax通过只使用k个负样本来降低成本至O(nk)，但在固定预算B=nk下，究竟应该优先增大批量还是增加负样本数量，这个问题此前尚不明确。

Method: 作者在标准平滑性和方差假设下对采样softmax训练进行了理论分析，推导出在固定内存约束下收敛速度与批量大小n和负样本数k的分配关系，并通过受控合成实验及四个真实序列推荐基准（包括MovieLens-20M）进行验证。

Result: 理论分析和实验结果一致表明，在固定预算下选择n~B、k~1（即尽量增大批量、减少负样本）可获得最快的收敛速度，且比不均衡的配置能获得更好的最终推荐质量。

Conclusion: 在内存预算固定时，应优先将资源用于增大批量大小而非增加负样本数量，这为推荐系统训练时内存配置提供了理论和实证依据。

Abstract: Large-scale neural recommender systems are typically trained with a softmax cross-entropy objective over the full item vocabulary. For a typical large number of possible items $K$, the final classification layer dominates memory, requiring $O(nK)$ logits and gradients to materialize for a batch of $n$ examples. Sampled softmax reduces this cost by restricting the objective to only $k \ll K$ candidate negative items, resulting in an $O(nk)$ memory. However, for a fixed budget $B = n k$, it remains unclear whether one should prioritize larger batches or the inclusion of more negative items.
  We address this question by analyzing sampled-softmax training under a fixed memory constraint. Under standard smoothness and variance assumptions, our theoretical evidence suggests that the fastest convergence arises from an $ n \sim B, k \sim 1$ allocation. So, an actionable rule is to include as many objects as possible given computational constraints.
  Our theory is supported by controlled synthetic and synthetic and four real sequential recommendation benchmarks, including MovieLens-20M. The suggested configuration achieve faster convergence and better final recommendation quality than imbalanced alternatives within the same memory constraint. These findings provide a theoretical and empirical foundation for configuring memory during the training of recommender systems. Code, reproducibility materials, and all scripts for generating figures are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LimitedMemoryRule-BBFB

</details>


### [77] [Cross-View Feature Matching: Survey, Benchmarking, and Foundation-Model Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11093)
*Songlin Du,Xiaoyong Lu,Zeyu Wu,Xiaobo Lu,Guobao Xiao,Bin Fan,Jiayi Ma,Takeshi Ikenaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对跨视角特征匹配领域进行了系统综述，提出了涵盖特征提取、单类型/多类型特征匹配器、视觉基础模型方法、训练策略和鲁棒估计的结构化分类体系，并在统一协议下对代表性方法进行了公平基准测试，同时探讨了效率、极端条件下的鲁棒性和跨域泛化等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角特征匹配领域在过去十年中经历了从任务特定模型到统一可泛化模型的演变，但现有研究在问题定义、模型架构、训练范式和评估协议等方面高度多样化，缺乏统一的领域理解框架，亟需系统性的综述来整合和梳理。

Method: 通过提出结构化分类体系（涵盖特征提取、单类型特征匹配器、多类型特征匹配器、视觉基础模型方法、训练策略和鲁棒估计）来组织现有研究，并设计统一实验基准，在一致的评估协议下对代表性最先进方法进行公平比较。

Result: 梳理了跨视角特征匹配的发展演变趋势，提炼了关键设计原则，突出了向统一可泛化对应模型的转变方向，并通过统一基准测试提供了代表性方法的公平性能对比结果。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解跨视角特征匹配在视觉基础模型时代的演变、当前格局和未来发展方向提供了全面且结构化的参考，并指出了效率、极端条件鲁棒性和跨域泛化等未来研究重点。

Abstract: Cross-view feature matching aims to establish reliable correspondences across images with large viewpoint variations. Over the past decade, the field has evolved from task-specific models toward increasingly unified and generalizable correspondence models, with recent progress further driven by the emergence of vision foundation models (VFMs). Despite these advances, existing studies remain highly diverse in their problem formulations, model architectures, training paradigms, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to obtain a unified understanding of the field. In this survey, we present a unified review of cross-view feature matching. We first introduce a structured taxonomy covering feature extraction, single-type feature matcher, multi-type feature matcher, VFMs based methods, training strategy and robust estimation, providing a coherent framework for analysis and comparison. We further examine recent advances, distilling key design principles and highlighting the shift toward unified and generalizable correspondence models. We also provide a unified experimental benchmarking of representative state-of-the-art methods under consistent protocols, enabling fair and comprehensive performance comparisons. In addition, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including efficiency, robustness under extreme conditions, and cross-domain generalization. This survey aims to provide a comprehensive and structured reference for understanding the evolution, current landscape, and future development of cross-view feature matching in the era of vision foundation models.

</details>


### [78] [Two-stage Odd Residual Flows for Mean-Preserving Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11114)
*Kiran Madhusudhanan,Christian Klötergens,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段奇残差流框架（TORF），将均值预测与不确定性估计解耦：第一阶段用预训练确定性模型获得精确均值，第二阶段用严格奇函数的受限归一化流学习残差分布，在不采样的情况下保证均值保持，从而同时实现高精度点预测和灵活的概率密度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测方法面临分布灵活性与均值预测精度之间的权衡：传统参数化方法（如MVE）在联合NLL训练下点预测精度下降，而现代生成模型（如NF和Diffusion）依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样且均值估计欠佳。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：（1）第一阶段使用预训练确定性模型预测精确均值；（2）第二阶段构建受限归一化流，其变换函数采用严格奇函数，学习点预测周围的灵活残差分布，从而在无需采样的条件下保证第一阶段的均值得以保持。

Result: 实验表明TORF在短时和长时预测任务中达到最先进的确定性精度（NMAE），同时在密度估计性能（CRPS）方面表现强劲。

Conclusion: TORF有效解决了概率预测中均值精度与分布灵活性之间的固有矛盾，通过解耦设计和奇函数约束实现了两方面的同时最优，为风险敏感的长时决策提供了更可靠的预测基础。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting plays an essential role in risk-sensitive decision-making, particularly in long-horizon settings. However, existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between distributional flexibility and accurate mean prediction. Traditional parametric methods, such as Mean Variance Estimation (MVE), can suffer from degraded point accuracy when trained under joint Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) objectives, while modern-flexible generative models, including Normalizing Flows and Diffusion Models, typically rely on costly Monte Carlo sampling and may yield suboptimal mean estimates. To address this limitation, we propose Two-stage Odd Residual Flows (TORF), a framework that decouples mean forecasting from uncertainty estimation. In the first stage, a pre-trained deterministic model is used to produce an accurate mean prediction. In the second stage, a Restricted Normalizing Flow, with strictly odd functions learns flexible residual distributions around the point forecast, guaranteeing mean preservation from the first stage without sampling. Experiments show that TORF achieves state-of-the-art deterministic accuracy (NMAE) while providing strong density estimation performance (CRPS) on short and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [79] [A Recommendation System Approach for Interference-Robust Sensor Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11143)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Kyle Pak,Merve Karakas,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于推荐系统思想的传感器子集选择方法，利用频带声学特征和双塔多层感知机（MLP）架构来替代传统的RSSI方法，在户外车辆跟踪部署中相比RSSI基线提升了约20%的跟踪精度，同时保持了实时选择性感知所需的低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于RSSI的传感器子集选择方法虽然高效，但容易受到声学干扰的影响，导致跟踪精度下降。需要一种既能保持低计算开销又能提升鲁棒性的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个受推荐系统启发的框架，利用频带声学特征替代RSSI测量，并采用双塔多层感知机（Two-Tower MLP）架构来高效地对候选传感器子集进行评分和选择，从而确定哪些传感器的昂贵感知模态（如摄像头）应被激活以实现高精度跟踪。

Result: 在户外车辆跟踪部署实验中，所提出的方法相比RSSI基线实现了约20%的精度提升，同时保持了实时选择性感知所需的低计算开销。

Conclusion: 频带声学特征结合双塔MLP架构能够有效克服RSSI方法受声学干扰的局限，在保证计算效率的同时显著提升传感器子集选择的跟踪精度，为实时选择性感知提供了一种可行且更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper develops a method for sensor-subset selection for tracking. Prior work showed that low-cost acoustic Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) measurements can be used to recommend subsets of sensor nodes whose expensive sensing modalities, such as cameras, can achieve high tracking accuracy. While efficient, RSSI-based approaches are challenged by acoustic interference. We propose a recommendation-system-inspired framework that instead leverages frequency-band acoustic features and a Two-Tower Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) architecture to efficiently score candidate sensor subsets. Experimental results on outdoor vehicle-tracking deployments show that the proposed method can improve accuracy by around 20\% over the RSSI baseline while maintaining the low computational overhead required for real-time selective sensing.

</details>


### [80] [DACRI: Decision-Aware Causal Intervention Ranking for Critical Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11154)
*Shiqi Huang,Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Jize Li,Yan Lyu,Lashimi Muraleedharan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CriticalSCM-Bench v1，一个带因果真值、事实/反事实轨迹和显式净价值目标的受控合成基准，用以评估供应链中断干预选择的净价值优化效果。研究发现LambdaMART在半导体和关键材料场景相比全信息静态基准提升5.7%-16.2%的中位归一化净价值，但在数字基础设施上领域知识常数缓冲策略仍更强；部分延迟设置下保持33%-75%的价值，且干预保真度、时机、成本等会改变策略排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测或归因供应链中断并不等同于选择能最大化可恢复净价值的干预措施。现有工作缺乏统一的基准来区分这两个问题，因此需要一个具有因果真值和显式净价值目标的受控环境来系统性评估干预选择策略。

Method: 构建CriticalSCM-Bench v1合成基准，包含因果ground truth、配对的事实/反事实rollout以及显式净价值目标函数。在该基准上对比LambdaMART（自适应排名模型）与全信息静态基准策略、领域知识常数缓冲策略，并进行部分/延迟信息设置、压力测试和受保护的解释生成研究（540次生成）。

Result: LambdaMART在半导体和关键材料场景中比静态基准提升5.7%-16.2%中位归一化净价值（有配对统计支持），但在数字基础设施场景不如常数缓冲策略。部分和延迟设置下保持33%-75%的完整钳位价值。压力测试显示干预保真度、时机、成本和未见过中断类型会改变策略排序；关键材料的外分布保持最弱。解释研究中540次生成在确定性验证和模板回退后保持全部固定干预决策，但措辞不稳定。

Conclusion: 在该受控环境中，自适应排名（如LambdaMART）在特定领域（半导体、关键材料）能显著增加价值，但并非均匀优于所有场景；在数字基础设施中更简单的结构化策略仍更优。研究界定了自适应排名有效和简单结构策略更优的不同制度，提示模型复杂度的提升需要场景化论证而非普遍适用。

Abstract: Detecting or attributing a supply-chain disruption is not the same as selecting the intervention that maximizes recoverable net value. We present CriticalSCM-Bench v1, a controlled synthetic benchmark with causal ground truth, paired factual/counterfactual rollouts, and an explicit net-value objective. Relative to a full-information train-selected static benchmark, LambdaMART improves median normalized net value by 5.7--16.2\%, with paired statistical support on the semiconductor and critical-material archetypes but not on digital infrastructure. On digital infrastructure, a domain-informed constant-buffer policy remains stronger, showing that greater model complexity is not uniformly justified. Across partial and delayed settings, LambdaMART retains 33--75\% of full-clamp value. Stress tests further show that intervention fidelity, timing, cost, and held-out disruptions can alter policy ordering. Critical materials show the weakest out-of-distribution retention. Separately, a guarded explanation study over 540 generations preserves every fixed intervention decision after deterministic validation and template fallback, although exact wording remains unstable. Within this controlled setting, the results identify regimes in which adaptive ranking adds value and those in which simpler structural policies remain preferable.

</details>


### [81] [Hierarchical Empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes: Minimax Smoothing and Calibration with AODE Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11162)
*Nguyen Thai Anh,Truong Viet Vu,Tran Thien Thanh,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Ngo Hoang Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层经验贝叶斯朴素贝叶斯（HEB-NB）及其一阶依赖扩展（HEB-AODE），通过Type-II极大似然自适应学习Dirichlet先验浓度，解决了传统平滑规则忽略特征基数、样本量和类别不平衡导致的偏差问题。理论分析建立了非渐近误差界、极大贝叶斯风险界及ECE推论，在31个基准数据集上取得最优平均Friedman排名，高基数数据上log-loss最高降低22.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NB分类器的平滑规则（如Laplace、Lidstone、K-T及m估计）均使用固定平滑强度，忽略了特征基数、样本量和类别不平衡等因素，导致在现代高基数表格数据上产生不可忽视的偏差，需要一种数据自适应的平滑策略。

Method: 提出HEB-NB，对每个类别-特征条件概率使用Dirichlet先验，通过Type-II极大似然自适应学习先验浓度参数，实现跨类别的信息共享同时保持闭式推理。进一步将自适应平滑扩展到AODE结构松弛模型（HEB-AODE），并可与互信息加权结合以降低校准误差。

Result: 在31个UCI和OpenML基准数据集上，HEB-NB在概率指标上取得最优平均Friedman排名，高基数数据集上log-loss最高降低22.1%，HEB-AODE相比原始AODE一致改进；结合互信息加权后top-1 ECE降低41%-70%。理论上建立了与经验分布极小极大速率匹配的l1误差界及Laplace严格分离的下界。

Conclusion: 数据自适应的经验贝叶斯平滑方法显著优于固定平滑规则，不仅在理论上保证偏差消失，还在实践中大幅提升概率准确性和校准质量，且能自然扩展到NB的结构松弛模型（如AODE）。

Abstract: The Naive Bayes (NB) classifier remains a standard choice for categorical data, yet its widely used smoothing rules, such as Laplace, Lidstone, Krichevsky-Trofimov, and the $m$-estimate, all prescribe a fixed smoothing strength that ignores feature cardinality, sample size, and class imbalance, inducing a non-vanishing bias on modern high-cardinality tabular data. We propose hierarchical empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes (HEB-NB), in which each class-feature conditional probability is smoothed by a Dirichlet prior whose concentration is learned data-adaptively via Type-II maximum likelihood, enabling principled information sharing across classes while retaining closed-form inference. We further introduce HEB average one-dependence estimators (HEB-AODE), showing that the adaptive smoothing transfers cleanly to structural relaxations of NB. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic $\ell_1$ error bound for HEB-NB matching the empirical-distribution minimax rate plus a vanishing data-adaptive bias, together with a matching Laplace-tight lower bound that yields a finite-sample, risk-level strict separation from Laplace. We further derive a plug-in excess Bayes-risk bound via total-variation tensorization and a population top-1 expected calibration error (ECE) corollary. Empirically, across 31 UCI and OpenML benchmarks, HEB-NB attains the best average Friedman rank on probabilistic metrics, with up to 22.1% log-loss reductions on high-cardinality datasets and consistent improvements of HEB-AODE over vanilla AODE. Combining HEB-NB with mutual-information weighting reduces top-1 ECE by 41%-70%, demonstrating substantial gains in probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond a Bag of Features: Set-Level Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11197)
*Nikolai Bolik,Lennart Stöpler,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Shani等人（2026）关于LLM表征与人类概念结构对齐的研究，使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）的激活潜变量集合重叠度作为更可解释的相似性度量。结果发现SAE激活集合并不能比稠密嵌入或残差流状态更忠实地恢复人类类别边界和典型性结构，而是追踪模型内部的相似性结构。进一步研究表明，在受控语义修改下，SAE激活集合的变化与人类对概念变化的判断存在显著不匹配，说明在理想化设定之外，SAE特征并不能通过简单的词袋语义进行组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅使用余弦相似度分析模型表征与人类概念结构的关系，作者希望引入SAE激活潜变量集合作为更可解释的相似性度量，以检验LLM表征是否真正反映人类类别边界和典型性结构。

Method: 使用SAE激活潜变量集合的重叠度作为相似性度量，在受控玩具模型和自然文本中验证该度量有效性，并将该度量应用于人类概念分析，同时在受控语义修改下研究激活集合变化与人类概念变化判断的对应关系。

Result: SAE激活集合能恢复受控玩具模型中的联合组合结构并在自然文本中产生语义连贯的邻域，但无法比稠密嵌入或残差流状态更忠实地恢复人类类别边界或内部典型性；SAE激活集合与人类概念变化判断存在显著不匹配。

Conclusion: 在理想化设定之外，SAE特征不能通过简单的词袋特征语义进行组合，SAE激活集合更多反映模型内部相似性结构而非人类概念结构。

Abstract: Shani et al. (2026) show that LLM representations broadly recover human category boundaries, while failing to reflect fine-grained typicality structure. Their analysis uses cosine similarity over dense model representations. We revisit their approach using overlap over active sparse autoencoder (SAE) latent sets as a more interpretable similarity measure. We first verify that this set-level measure is meaningful: SAE latent sets can recover union-like compositional structure in controlled toy models and induce semantically coherent neighborhoods in natural text. Extending the human-concepts analysis to SAE set similarities, we find that SAE activation sets do not recover human category boundaries or within-category typicality more faithfully than dense embeddings or residual-stream states, but instead track model-internal similarity structure. To probe this gap further, we study active latent sets under well-controlled semantic modifications, revealing a substantial mismatch between human judgements of conceptual change and change in the SAE active set. We interpret this as evidence that, outside idealised settings, SAE features do not compose via simple bag-of-features semantics.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Hand-Written PTX Tensor-Core GEMM Kernels: A Multi-Precision Study on NVIDIA L4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10103)
*Matt J. Borowski,Blazej Osinski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在NVIDIA L4 GPU上通过受控实验对比了WMMA C++ API与手写PTX的GEMM内核性能，发现FP16下PTX无优势，但INT8和INT4下分别获得1.4x-1.8x和2.9x-4.3x加速，并指出占用率不是吞吐量的良好预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数应用代码通过WMMA C++ API间接访问Tensor Core，但高性能内核依赖底层PTX管线。作者希望回答一个实际问题：何时用手写PTX替代WMMA才能真正带来性能收益，从而帮助开发者在复杂性和性能之间做出合理权衡。

Method: 在NVIDIA L4 GPU（Ada架构）上进行了受控单GPU实验，对比双缓冲WMMA基线内核与一系列手写PTX GEMM内核，覆盖FP16、INT8、INT4三种精度和N=512到N=8192的方阵规模，使用Nsight Compute对所有内核进行全量指标剖析。

Result: FP16下PTX无端到端加速（指令级收益被操作数打包开销抵消）；INT8下PTX获得1.4x-1.8x加速（源于更低指令数和更好的全局内存合并）；INT4下获得2.9x-4.3x加速（原生mma.sync.m16n8k64.s4避免了WMMA的软件模拟）。相对FP16 WMMA基线，最佳量化内核在N=8192时INT8达34.4x、INT4达98.7x。大矩阵下性能更受内存系统行为（全局加载合并和DRAM活跃周期）而非Tensor Core利用率驱动。

Conclusion: 手写PTX并非在所有精度下都值得，其额外复杂度仅在INT8和INT4量化场景中合理；FP16场景下应继续使用WMMA。此外，性能分析不应过度依赖占用率指标，而应关注内存系统行为。

Abstract: High-performance Tensor Core kernels rely on a low-level PTX pipeline built from asynchronous data movement with cp.async, warp-level matrix loads with ldmatrix, and matrix multiply-accumulate operations with mma.sync. However, most application code accesses Tensor Cores indirectly through the WMMA C++ API. This paper asks a focused, practical question: when does replacing WMMA with hand-written PTX actually pay off? To answer this question, we conduct a controlled, single-GPU study on an NVIDIA L4 GPU (Ada, SM89), comparing double-buffered WMMA baselines with a family of hand-written PTX GEMM kernels across FP16, INT8, and INT4 arithmetic and square problem sizes from $N=512$ to $N=8192$. Every kernel is profiled with Nsight Compute across the full metric set, and PTX speedups are reported relative to the corresponding same-precision WMMA baseline. Hand-written PTX provides no end-to-end speedup for FP16, because its instruction-level gains are offset by operand-packing overhead. In contrast, the PTX kernels achieve consistent speedups of 1.4x-1.8x for INT8, driven primarily by lower instruction counts and better global-memory coalescing, and 2.9x-4.3x for INT4, where native mma.sync.m16n8k64.s4 execution avoids the software-emulated sequence used by the WMMA path. Relative to the FP16 WMMA baseline, the best quantized kernels reach 34.4x (INT8) and 98.7x (INT4) at $N=8192$. Across these experiments, occupancy is a poor predictor of throughput. For large matrices, performance instead tracks memory-system behavior -- particularly global-load coalescing and DRAM-active cycles -- more closely than Tensor Core utilization. These results identify the precisions and operating regimes in which the additional complexity of hand-written PTX is justified.

</details>


### [84] [What Actually Serializes GPU LZ77 Decode: Three Decoders, Three Mechanisms, and an Encode-Time Lever That Removes the Last One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10188)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过H100上的多架构实验，推翻了对GPU LZ77解码顺序瓶颈的普遍假设：解析（parse）而非拷贝（copy）占解码时间64-72%；通过证明并限制回引链深度（仅损失0.006%压缩率）对延迟影响极小；自重叠匹配本质上是周期性填充而非依赖链，可完全并行化，匹配层提速2.75-8.42倍；编码器可移除四项距离历史这一最后顺序元素（代价0.540%压缩率），使无依赖解析运行从4条指令增至706条。同时报告格式本身的带宽瓶颈（缓存行效率仅4.4%），并记录十个被实验推翻的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍假设GPU LZ77解码的顺序瓶颈在于拷贝阶段，本文旨在通过严格的实证测量重新定位真正的瓶颈所在，并用机器可验证的方式支撑每一项结论，挑战该领域的既有认知。

Method: 在H100上构建三种解码器架构进行对比实验；对全部15,499个块进行字节级比较以证明回引链深度上限不改变任何涉及块；利用自重叠匹配的周期性填充结构实现完全并行化；通过编码器端移除四项距离历史来扩大无依赖解析范围；并对每种可复现声明提供无需GPU即可通过的机器检查记录。

Result: 解析阶段占设备驻留解码时间的64-72%；回引链深度上限（损失0.006%压缩率）对延迟影响最多2.8%，且不改变涉及延迟尖峰的181个块；自重叠匹配并行化使匹配层提速2.75-8.42倍且位精确；移除四项距离历史（损失0.540%压缩率）将无依赖解析从4条指令扩展至706条；中位匹配7字节对128字节缓存行的总线效率仅4.4%，合并写入快39倍；17项可复现检查全部通过。

Conclusion: GPU LZ77解码的真正顺序瓶颈是解析阶段而非拷贝阶段，且该瓶颈可被系统性消除或大幅缓解；但格式本身受限于缓存行利用率的物理下限，这是比顺序依赖更根本的性能天花板。

Abstract: The sequential part of GPU LZ77 decode is not where the field assumes it is. Across three decoder architectures on an H100 we measure that parse, not copy, holds 64-72% of device-resident decode time; that bounding back-reference chain depth - provable, and costing 0.006% in ratio - moves latency by at most 2.8% and, for the file's own latency spike, provably by nothing at all, since a byte-level comparison of all 15,499 blocks shows the cap alters none of the 181 blocks involved; that self-overlapping matches are periodic fills rather than dependency chains, which makes them fully parallel and speeds the match layer by 2.75-8.42x bit-perfect; and that the last genuinely sequential element, a four-entry distance history, can be removed by the encoder for 0.540% of ratio, growing the dependency-free parse run from 4 commands to 706. We also report the floor the format runs into: with a median match of 7 bytes against a 128-byte cache line, bus efficiency is 4.4% and a coalesced write of the same data is 39x faster. A separate section records ten hypotheses these measurements refuted, including one methodological error of our own. Every reproducible claim carries a machine-checkable record: a fresh clone of the tagged release passes 17 of 17 checks reachable without a GPU, none failing.

</details>


### [85] [FaCTz: Fast Critical-Point and Topology-Aware GPU Compression for Scientific Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10586)
*Mingze Xia,Yuxiao Li,Sheng Di,Jiannan Tian,Baixi Sun,Boyi Zhang,Bei Wang,Hanqi Guo,Xin Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FaCTz，这是首个基于GPU的、保证临界点保持的误差有界有损压缩器。其核心洞察是将临界点保持这一本质上有序的约束重构为独立的并行任务（逐块或投机逐点）。FaCTz在三个向量场数据集上实现了高达60GB/s的吞吐量，比多线程CPU实现cpSZ快约两个数量级（最高约640倍），同时保证所有临界点得到保持，其投机模式相比吞吐量优先模式压缩比提升约一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学模拟产生的向量场数据需要误差有界有损压缩来存储和传输，但现有压缩器虽然限制数值误差，却不保持场的拓扑结构——微小的容许扰动可能创建或消除下游特征分析所依赖的临界点。现有的GPU压缩器吞吐量高但与拓扑无关，而唯一具有可证明临界点保持能力的cpSZ在CPU上运行，吞吐量远低于现代GPU系统的数据生成速率。

Method: FaCTz的核心方法是将临界点保持这一本质上耦合且顺序化的约束重新表述为独立的并行任务，通过两种模式实现：(1) 逐块模式，针对吞吐量优化；(2) 投机逐点模式，针对压缩比优化。这是首个在GPU上保证临界点保持的误差有界有损压缩器。

Result: 在三个向量场数据集上，FaCTz保持所有临界点的同时实现了高达60GB/s的吞吐量，比多线程CPU实现的cpSZ快约两个数量级（最高约640倍）。其投机模式相比吞吐量优先模式，压缩比进一步提升了约一倍。

Conclusion: FaCTz证明了临界点保持这一看似顺序化的约束可以成功并行化并在GPU上高效实现，在保证拓扑正确性的同时实现了远超CPU方案的吞吐量，为现代GPU驱动的科学计算系统提供了可行的拓扑保持压缩方案。

Abstract: Error-bounded lossy compression is essential for storing and transferring the vector-field data produced by large-scale scientific simulations. Although it enforces a user-specified error bound to limit numerical distortion, it does not preserve the field's topology: small admissible perturbations can create or eliminate critical points on which downstream feature analysis depends. Existing GPU compressors achieve high throughput but are topology-agnostic, whereas the only compressor with provable critical-point preservation (cpSZ) runs on the CPU at throughput far below the data-generation rates of modern GPU-based systems. We observe that, although preserving critical points is inherently a coupled and sequential constraint, it can be reformulated into independent parallel tasks, either on a per-block basis or, speculatively, on a per-point basis. We present FaCTz, the first GPU-based error-bounded lossy compressor that guarantees critical-point preservation. FaCTz provides a block-wise mode optimized for throughput and a speculative per-point mode optimized for compression ratio. Across three vector-field datasets, FaCTz preserves every critical point while achieving throughput of up to 60 GB/s, approximately two orders of magnitude (up to approximately 640x) faster than the multithreaded CPU implementation of cpSZ. Its speculative mode further improves the compression ratio by approximately a factor of two over the throughput-oriented mode.

</details>


### [86] [ClusterBench: A Framework for Cluster-Wide Continuous Benchmarking and Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10956)
*Aditya Ujeniya,Jan Eitzinger,Thomas Gruber,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ClusterBench，一个面向数据中心集群的连续基准测试框架，能够在单次提交中将相同基准分发到所有节点，对CPU、GPU、内存、互连和I/O等组件进行集群级验证，并通过跨空间和时间的数据收集检测性能回归、支持硬件变异性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心需要能够验证整个安装而非单个节点的工具，应用于验收测试及之后的定期检查。这要求将相同基准分发给每个节点并在单次提交中完成，因此需要支持集群感知调度。

Method: ClusterBench框架实现集群级连续基准测试，包含覆盖各组件（CPU、GPU、内存、互连、I/O）的基准集合。测量在集群整个生命周期内重复进行，从而跨空间和时间收集数据；通过与历史运行结果对比检测性能回归，并将测量数据作为硬件变异性研究的数据集。

Result: 在NHR@FAU集群（Helma、Alex和Fritz）上，同一组件内部的性能变化保持在1%以内，而不同节点之间的差异达到5%（即使规格完全相同的节点）。性能与功耗、频率和温度的相关性分析表明，风冷节点和液冷节点之间存在差异。

Conclusion: ClusterBench能够有效进行集群级连续基准测试，检测软件变更引发的性能回归，并为研究硬件变异性提供有价值的数据集。同时揭示了同规格节点间存在显著性能差异，以及冷却方式对性能-功耗-温度关系的影响。

Abstract: Data centers need tooling that validates an entire installation rather than individual nodes, at acceptance and at regular intervals thereafter. This requires dispatching identical benchmarks to every node in a single submission, and therefore cluster-aware scheduling. This paper presents ClusterBench, a framework for cluster-wide continuous benchmarking. It ships with a benchmark collection targeting each component: CPU, GPU, memory, interconnect, and I/O. Because measurements are repeated throughout the cluster's lifetime, ClusterBench collects data across space and time. Comparison against earlier runs detects performance regressions introduced by software changes, such as kernel updates or new library versions. The measurements also form a dataset for research on hardware variability. On the NHR@FAU clusters Helma, Alex, and Fritz, variation within a single component stays within 1%. Variation across specimens reaches 5%, despite nodes identical by specification. Correlating performance with power draw, frequency, and temperature shows that this relationship differs between air- and liquid-cooled nodes.

</details>


### [87] [Workflow Cards: Structured Summaries of Workflow Executions Using Provenance Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11022)
*Nicola Giuseppe Marchioro,Gabriele Padovani,Amal Gueroudji,Rafael Ferreira da Silva,Wesley Brewer,Valentine Anantharaj,Sandro Fiore,Renan Souza*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Workflow Cards——一种结构化文档卡片，用于记录机器学习工作流执行过程的溯源信息，填补Model Cards和Data Cards只关注静态产物而忽视执行过程细节的空白。实验表明Workflow Cards能提供执行级信息，且相比基于schema的数据库查询，LLM对Workflow Cards的理解质量几乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Model Cards和Data Cards仅关注静态工件（数据集和训练后的模型），忽略了产生、转换和评估这些工件的工作流执行过程。然而，数据准备、参数选择、运行行为、资源使用及中间转换等关键细节恰恰是偏差、性能变异和可复现性缺口产生的根源。

Method: 论文包含两部分：首先，定义了一个Workflow Card模板，该模板基于从Model和Data Cards中缺失的执行级数据中提炼的一组代表性溯源问题；其次，评估LLM使用Workflow Cards理解工作流执行的效果，与通过基于schema的接口查询溯源数据库的方式进行比较。

Result: 结果显示：（1）Workflow Cards提供了Model Cards和Data Cards等现有卡片类型所缺失的执行级信息，填补了重要的文档空白；（2）与基于schema的查询方式相比，Workflow Cards使答案质量几乎翻倍，且这一结论在LLM-as-a-Judge和人工评估中一致成立。

Conclusion: Workflow Cards作为一种新颖的文档形式，能够将机器可读的工作流执行溯源数据压缩为人类和LLM都能阅读分析的结构化总结，有效弥合了现有ML文档实践在执行层面上的缺口，并显著提升LLM对工作流执行的理解能力。

Abstract: Model Cards and Data Cards have demonstrated the value of structured, human-readable documentation for machine learning artifacts, capturing their context, parameters, limitations, and intended use. However, these practices remain focused on static artifacts (the datasets and trained models themselves) while overlooking the workflow executions that produce, transform, and evaluate them. Such executions hold critical details about data preparation, parameter choice, runtime behavior, resource use, and intermediate transformations, precisely where bias, performance variation, and reproducibility gaps tend to originate. To close this gap, we introduce Workflow Cards: structured summaries that condense the machine-readable provenance data of a workflow execution into a form both humans and large language models (LLMs) can read and analyze. This paper has two main parts. First, it defines a Workflow Card template informed by a representative set of provenance questions that surface from the execution-level data missing from Model and Data Cards. Second, it evaluates how effectively LLMs use Workflow Cards to understand workflow executions compared with querying provenance databases through a schema-based interface. Results show that Workflow Cards provide execution-level information absent from existing card types, such as Model Cards and Data Cards, thereby filling an important documentation gap; and that Workflow Cards nearly double answer quality compared with schema-based querying, consistently across LLM-as-a-Judge and human assessments.

</details>


### [88] [SCOUT: Symmetric Consensus Outlier Detection for Failure Localization in LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11034)
*Zhuang Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个统一的LLM预训练运行时故障定位框架，利用严格多数共识识别异常副本，通过带外CPU观察器、原位重放和集体指纹等技术，在不修改训练循环或框架源码的前提下，实现对大模型训练中卡顿、数值错误和静默数据损坏的快速定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中，同步机制会将秩局部的卡顿、慢速和数值错误传播为整个作业范围的症状，掩盖了故障的根源。现有诊断手段要么依赖进程内监视器（训练阻塞或终止后无法报告），要么依赖仅保留同步症状的事后日志，而离线健康测试又丢失了触发故障的工作负载和运行条件，导致故障定位困难。

Method: SCOUT基于"通过等价副本间的严格多数共识识别异常"这一设计原则。它对齐副本的进度、时序和数值证据，利用C3（Consensus Collective Communication）抽象识别签名与同伴不一致的秩。具体包括：带外CPU观察器在训练挂起时保持响应；原位重放（in-situ replay）在任务运行过程中再现周期性拖尾者和静默数据损坏（SDC），保留模型状态、内核、内存分配、通信路径及热/内存压力；集体指纹暴露秩本地协议分歧；干净的覆盖重放验证检查点数值完整性。

Result: SCOUT能够有效定位由同步传播导致的秩本地故障，支持拖尾者、静默数据损坏和协议分歧的识别，且能防止从被SDC污染的检查点恢复。该框架与PyTorch、TorchTitan、Megatron-Core和DeepSpeed无缝集成，无需修改训练循环或框架源代码，并已开源发布。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过带外观察、原位重放和共识机制的统一设计，为大模型预训练提供了一套无需侵入式修改的运行时故障定位解决方案，在保障检查点数值完整性的同时显著提升了故障诊断的时效性和准确性。

Abstract: In LLM pre-training, synchronization propagates rank-local stalls, slowdowns, and numerical errors into job-wide symptoms, obscuring their origin. Existing diagnosis often relies on in-process monitors that cannot report after the trainer blocks or terminates, or on post-mortem logs that preserve only synchronized symptoms; offline health tests lose the workload and operating conditions that triggered the failure. We present SCOUT, a unified runtime failure-localization framework built on one design principle: identify outliers through strict-majority consensus among equivalent replicas. SCOUT aligns replica progress, timing, and numerical evidence, then uses its Consensus Collective Communication (C3) abstraction to identify ranks whose compact signatures disagree with their peers. An out-of-band CPU observer remains responsive when training hangs, whereas in-situ replay exercises recurring stragglers and silent data corruption (SDC) beside the live job with its model state, kernels, allocations, communication path, and thermal and memory pressure present. Collective fingerprints expose rank-local protocol divergence. Clean replay coverage certifies checkpoint numerical integrity, preventing recovery from selecting state corrupted by SDC. SCOUT integrates with PyTorch, TorchTitan, Megatron-Core, and DeepSpeed without training-loop or framework-source modifications. SCOUT is open source at https://github.com/LMResiliency/lm-resiliency.

</details>


### [89] [Scheduling Mixed RL Rollouts Beyond Prefix Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11152)
*Zetao Hong,Song Yuan,Yuanhao Ding,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Zhibin Wang,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MISA-T，一种面向混合rollout服务的路由层准入策略，通过自适应会话准入、工作负载感知的KV容量分配和驻留时间感知的KV记账，在不扭曲训练器指定的工作负载混合的前提下，显著提升LLM强化学习后训练管线中异构rollout会话的推理效率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM的强化学习后训练管线中，RLVR、RLHF和agentic rollout共享异步推理服务，其序列结构、交互模式和KV驻留时间差异巨大，导致服务需求截然不同。现有的前缀感知路由虽然能改善缓存重用和负载均衡，但无法控制异构rollout会话对KV缓存容量的竞争，rollout调度需要在这种异构性下仍保持训练器指定的工作负载混合。

Method: MISA-T是一种路由层准入策略，包含三个关键组件：1）自适应会话准入控制；2）工作负载感知的KV容量分配；3）驻留时间感知的KV记账。该系统动态调节进入服务的rollout会话数量和KV缓存分配，以兼顾吞吐量与工作负载保真度。

Result: 在Step3.7和Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上的rollout消融实验中，相比超参数扫描调优的缓存感知vLLM Router，MISA-T分别提升rollout吞吐量53.3%和43.6%，同时保持高前缀缓存命中率。在匹配的50轮Step3.7实验中，吞吐量提升35.6%，平均迭代时间减少22.8%，消耗的工作负载混合接近训练器目标，任务得分相当。

Conclusion: MISA-T有效解决了混合异构rollout场景下的KV缓存资源竞争问题，在不损害训练质量和工作负载混合的前提下大幅提升推理效率，为大规模LLM强化学习后训练服务提供了实用的调度方案。

Abstract: Modern reinforcement learning (RL) post-training pipelines for large language models (LLMs) increasingly combine rollout workloads across multiple domains and feedback paradigms. Prefix-aware routing improves inference efficiency through cache reuse and load balancing, but it does not control how heterogeneous rollout sessions compete for KV-cache capacity. When reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), and agentic rollouts share an asynchronous inference service, their distinct sequence structures, interaction patterns, and KV-residency times create substantially different serving demands. Rollout scheduling must account for this heterogeneity without distorting the workload mixture specified by the trainer. We present MISA-T, a routing-layer admission policy for mixed rollout serving. MISA-T combines adaptive session admission, workload-aware KV-capacity allocation, and residency-time-aware KV accounting. In rollout-only ablations on Step3.7 and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, MISA-T improves rollout throughput over a sweep-tuned cache-aware vLLM Router by 53.3% and 43.6%, respectively, while maintaining high prefix-cache hit rates. In a matched 50-iteration Step3.7 experiment, it increases rollout throughput by 35.6% and reduces mean iteration time by 22.8%, while keeping the consumed workload mixture close to the trainer target and achieving comparable task scores.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [N2NMatcher: Towards Inlining-Resilient Binary Decomposition and Module Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10043)
*Ang Jia,He Jiang,Zhipeng Yang,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出N2NMatcher，一个对函数内联具有鲁棒性的二进制分解与模块匹配框架，通过学习预测稳定的锚点函数来实现跨编译设置的模块分解与匹配，提升程序级二进制代码相似性分析的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序级二进制代码相似性分析方法通常按函数调用图结构分解二进制为模块再匹配，但函数内联会同时改变调用图结构和函数语义，导致现有分解和匹配方法失效。

Method: 首先通过实证研究考察二进制中是否存在跨编译设置稳定的函数模块边界；然后使用层级图神经网络编码由操作码序列、控制流结构和调用上下文的ACFG-FCG表示，学习预测锚点函数；最后基于锚点进行受限分解，并使用学习到的模块图嵌入进行模块匹配。

Result: 实验表明，N2NMatcher在分解质量和模块匹配准确率上均优于现有方法，能够实现更有效的程序级BCSA。

Conclusion: N2NMatcher通过锚点函数预测和内联鲁棒的分解策略，有效解决了函数内联对程序级二进制代码相似性分析的干扰，验证了学习稳定模块边界和嵌入匹配的可行性。

Abstract: Program-level Binary Code Similarity Analysis (BCSA) aims to identify semantically similar code regions across binary programs, serving as a fundamental technique for software plagiarism detection, vulnerability search, and malware analysis. Existing approaches often decompose binaries into modules following the structure of function call graphs (FCGs) and then match these modules by their contained functions. However, function inlining changes both FCG structures and binary function semantics, making existing decomposition and module matching methods less effective.
  In this work, we propose N2NMatcher, an inlining-resilient framework for binary decomposition and module matching. We first conduct an empirical study to examine whether binaries still contain functions that provide stable module boundaries across compilation settings. N2NMatcher learns to predict such functions as anchors using a hierarchical graph neural network that encodes binary ACFG-FCG representations built from opcode sequences, control-flow structures, and FCG calling context. It then performs anchor-bounded decomposition and matches the generated modules using learned module graph embeddings. Experimental results show that N2NMatcher improves both the decomposition quality and module matching accuracy compared to existing works, enabling more effective program-level BCSA.

</details>


### [91] [Exploring Semantic Stability Across Reviews in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10101)
*Lucas Ciziks,Paulo Meirelles,Marco Aurélio Gerosa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对Linux IIO子系统的10,117个函数轨迹进行逐版本追踪，测量代码审查过程中函数在首次提交与最终合并版本之间的相似度。结果显示虽然表面相似度极高，但这主要是75.3%未经修改的函数导致的假象；聚焦于真实编辑过的函数后，语义保持度仍然很高（均值0.990 vs 基线0.909），但漂移集中在第一轮审查，且控制变量后仍存在统计上可检测但很小的残余效应。作者提出开放问题：近满分的相似度反映的是意图保留还是测量工具无法捕捉微小局部编辑的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往代码审查研究通常只分析最终合并的补丁，而不与首次提交进行比较，缺乏对补丁演进过程中函数级变化的量化理解。

Method: 采用函数级测量方法，追踪Linux IIO子系统补丁历史中10,117条函数轨迹（每条函数跨越一个补丁系列的编号修订），使用相似度分数与无关函数对作为基线进行对比分析。

Result: 75.3%的轨迹从未被文本修改（贡献100%相似度虚高标题数字）；剔除未编辑轨迹后，真实编辑轨迹的平均相似度为0.990（基线0.909）；漂移集中出现在第一轮审查，主要因后续轮次包含更多未修改函数而非编辑变得更保守；控制后仍有统计上可检测但很小的残余效应。

Conclusion: 代码审查可能主要保留函数的语义目的，但近满分相似度也可能反映了测量工具无法检测微小局部编辑重要性的局限，需要进一步研究以区分这两种解释。

Abstract: Code review is credited with substantially changing a patch's code between its first submission and the version that eventually lands. However, prior work typically studied only the final merged patch without comparing it to the first submission. We present a function-level measurement that tracks 10,117 trajectories (each function followed across the numbered revisions of one patch series) through the patch history of the Linux IIO subsystem, comparing similarity scores against unrelated function pairs as a baseline. A naive reading yields near-total similarity, but this is largely an artifact of composition: 75.3% of tracked trajectories are never textually modified between versions, contributing a trivial 100% similarity that inflates the headline. Restricting to the trajectories with a real edit, semantic purpose is still largely preserved (mean similarity 0.990 vs. a 0.909 baseline), but drift appears to concentrate in the first review round mainly because later rounds contain more functions that nobody touched, not because edits become more conservative over time. After controlling for it, a statistically detectable but small residual effect remains. This points to an open question: whether near-ceiling similarity reflects preserved purpose or a measurement tool that cannot detect the significance of small, localized edits. We present this work as a first look and outline next steps.

</details>


### [92] [One Recipe, Many Harnesses: What Self-Evolution Encodes Across Languages and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10178)
*Siqi Yang,Qianlan Yang,Yu-Xiong Wang,Saurabh Pujar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过固定进化配方，在8种编程语言和3个基础模型上系统分析自进化编码代理（self-evolving harness）中进化的产物究竟编码了什么。研究发现，进化收益主要来源于对模型可恢复执行缺陷的补偿，而非基准适配或语言特定知识；不同语言共享抽象操作手册但使用几乎不相交的语言生态系统机制；共享核心可蒸馏为通用harness，而生态系统边缘部分需要原生再进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅报告自进化harness的聚合性能提升，未深入分析进化产物究竟编码了什么——是基准特定适配、语言专精工程知识，还是对底层模型局限性的补偿？这一模糊性阻碍了对进化机制的理解和泛化能力的判断。

Method: 在Multi-SWE-Bench的8种编程语言×3个基础模型的网格上固定进化配方进行实验。进化过程中每个编辑都通过类型化失败信号（typed failure signal）路由，并记录为可证伪契约（falsifiable contract），使每个修改在进化后可归因。通过对比最小种子和手动设计的mini-SWE-agent scaffold，分析held-out解决率、缺陷质量分布、跨语言共享模式及蒸馏迁移能力。

Result: (1) 进化循环在多数单元格中提升held-out解决率，但存在两个零效应区域；(2) 收益与可恢复执行缺陷的消除相关，缺陷质量接近零时增益也接近零，且主导缺陷因单元而异；(3) 进化harness跨语言共享抽象操作手册，但实例化时使用几乎不相交的语言生态系统机制；(4) 共享核心可蒸馏为通用harness，而生态系统边缘红利无法迁移，需要原生再进化。

Conclusion: 自进化harness本质上是一个"可读的补偿层"（legible compensation layer），由语言的工程需求和模型的行为缺陷共同塑造，而非不透明的基准调优脚手架。其核心价值在于弥合策略模型"能做什么"与"实际做什么"之间的差距。

Abstract: Self-evolving harnesses are closed-loop systems in which an agent inspects its own rollouts and edits its prompts, tools, and memory. They reliably improve coding agents in evaluations, but prior work reports aggregate gains rather than analyzing what the evolved artifacts encode. It therefore remains unclear whether they encode benchmark-specific adaptations, language-specific engineering knowledge, or compensation for limitations of the underlying model. We disentangle these factors by holding an evolution recipe fixed across a grid of eight programming languages (Multi-SWE-Bench) and three base models, and analyzing the resulting harnesses. The recipe routes every edit through a typed failure signal and records it as a falsifiable contract, making each modification attributable after evolution. Four findings emerge. (1)The loop improves held-out solve rates over both a minimal seed and the manually designed mini-SWE-agent scaffold in most cells, but with two null regions. (2)Gains compensate recoverable execution defects, where defect mass is near zero, and gain is near zero; which defect dominates is cell-specific. A harness closes the gap between what a policy can do and what it does. (3)Evolved harnesses share an abstract playbook across languages but instantiate it with almost disjoint language ecosystem machinery. (4)The shared core transfers and can be distilled into one universal harness, while an ecosystem margin resists both and requires native re-evolution. Together, these results recast the evolved harness as a legible compensation layer, shaped jointly by the language's engineering demands and the model's behavioral gaps, rather than an opaque benchmark-tuned scaffold.

</details>


### [93] [Bias Smells in AI Software Development: Recognizing Potential Sources of Fairness Debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10248)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究识别出AI软件项目中预示公平性债务的六种"偏差气味"指标，将软件气味范式扩展到公平性领域，为将公平性纳入软件质量保证提供可观察的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性债务源于AI开发缺陷可能导致的长期社会危害，但现有研究缺乏对从业者在开发过程中如何识别公平性债务潜在来源的经验证据。

Method: 对一家组织内的四个AI项目进行探索性案例研究，通过25名专业人士的半结构化访谈和开放式问卷收集数据，辅以内部沟通渠道和项目文档的观察，采用迭代定性编码、备忘录和持续比较法进行分析。

Result: 识别出六种反复出现的指标（即"偏差气味"）：上下文过度简化、数据集失衡、指标不充分、临时测试、个体多样性无知觉、同质化团队构成。这些气味涵盖软件开发和软件开发的技术与人文层面，预示可能引入或强化偏见并导致公平性债务的条件。

Conclusion: 偏差气味将软件气味范式扩展到公平性领域，为通过可观察指标将公平性纳入软件质量保证提供了基础。

Abstract: Context: Fairness debt arises from AI development shortcomings that may lead to societal harms. While technical and social debt concern software design decisions and team dynamics, respectively, fairness debt captures the long-term consequences of development decisions that may reinforce bias and inequities in AI-based software systems. Despite growing attention to AI fairness, limited evidence exists on how practitioners recognize potential sources of fairness debt during development. Aim: This study investigates the indicators practitioners recognize as signaling potential sources of fairness debt in AI-based software projects. Method: We conducted an exploratory case study of four AI projects within one organization. Data were collected from 25 professionals through semi-structured interviews and open-ended questionnaires, complemented by observation of internal communication channels and project documentation, and analyzed using iterative qualitative coding, memoing, and constant comparison. Results: We identified six recurring indicators, termed bias smells: Context Oversimplification, Dataset Imbalance, Metrics Inadequacies, Ad hoc Testing, Individual Diversity Unawareness, and Homogeneous Team Composition. These smells span technical and human aspects of software development and signal conditions that may introduce or reinforce bias and contribute to fairness debt. Conclusion: Bias smells extend the software smell paradigm to fairness and provide a foundation for incorporating fairness into software quality assurance through observable indicators.

</details>


### [94] [Enhancing Reliability of Symbolic Execution Tools for Smart Contract Analysis through Rule-Based False Positive Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10265)
*Muhammad Ali Hassan Ahmad,Muhammad Hashim Ali,Muhammad Ali Amer,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Muhammad Hassan,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对基于符号执行的以太坊智能合约漏洞分析工具Mythril产生大量误报的问题，分析了误报的根本原因，并提出了一种基于规则的方案来减少误报，在不影响真实漏洞检测率的前提下显著提升了工具可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约的安全漏洞检测工具（如基于符号执行的Mythril）常常产生大量误报，这些误报会浪费开发者的时间和精力，削弱工具的可靠性。因此，需要深入分析误报产生的原因，并改进算法和启发式规则以区分真实漏洞与误报。

Method: 本文首先分析Mythril生成的漏洞报告中的误报普遍性及其根本原因，然后基于分析所得洞察，设计并实现了一套基于规则的方法，针对Mythril中最具影响的几类漏洞应用这些规则，以减少误报。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于规则的方法在保持真实漏洞检测能力的前提下，显著减少了误报数量，从而提升了Mythril工具的整体可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过分析误报根源并设计针对性的规则来过滤误报，可以有效提升智能合约漏洞分析工具的可靠性和实用性，为后续工具改进提供了可行的方向。

Abstract: A blockchain is a decentralized, secure ledger system that enables transparent and immutable record-keeping, essential for trust and security in digital transactions. Smart contracts are self-executing agreements encoded on a blockchain, enabling different parties to fulfill the terms of the agreement automatically. These contracts trigger corresponding actions when conditions are met, ensuring decentralized and transparent transactions. Writing reliable smart contracts is challenging due to the lack of standardization. To find security vulnerabilities, tools based on various approaches, including symbolic execution, are used. However, these tools often report a large number of false positives, raising concerns about their reliability. The time and effort spent investigating false positives diverts resources from addressing actual vulnerabilities. Therefore, such tools must also be evaluated according to the rate of false positives they exhibit. More importantly, the algorithms and heuristics used by the tools must be enhanced to distinguish between true vulnerabilities and false alarms. In this paper, we first demonstrate the prevalence of false positives in vulnerability reports generated by Mythril, a symbolic execution-based analysis tool for Ethereum smart contracts. We analyze the root causes of these inaccuracies and devise a rule-based approach based on the gained insight to reduce false positives. We implement our rules for the most impactful vulnerabilities in Mythril and assess the effectiveness of our approach. Our results show a significant reduction in false positives without compromising the detection of true vulnerabilities, thus enhancing the tool's reliability.

</details>


### [95] [Comprendia: AI-Augmented Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10290)
*Costain Nachuma,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comprendia是一个Eclipse插件，通过集成的交互式依赖图和LLM代码解释来辅助Java程序理解，包含基于图感知的被调用者剪枝（GACP）的LLM解释、克隆检测覆盖层和CVE风险覆盖层四大核心功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序理解是软件开发中的关键任务，传统的结构依赖可视化与LLM代码解释通常相互独立，缺乏统一视图。作者希望通过将两者整合到共享交互式图上，兼顾结构感知与智能解释，同时确保LLM提示的可复现性和可审计性。

Method: Comprendia基于四个支柱实现：(1)多边类型的依赖图，支持实时搜索和多布局；(2)基于图感知的被调用者剪枝（GACP）策略生成LLM提示，该策略利用图距离、继承折叠和边类型加权来筛选相关被调用者；(3)克隆检测覆盖层用于突出重复代码并提出提取到父类的重构建议；(4)基于OSV.dev的CVE风险覆盖层。

Result: 作者在包含已知克隆和漏洞的Java项目上演示了Comprendia，展示了统一图基板如何支持程序理解同时保持开发者的控制权。工具的视频演示也已发布。

Conclusion: Comprendia通过将结构依赖图与LLM解释统一在共享交互式图上，实现了可审计、可复现的智能代码理解，并集成了克隆检测和CVE风险分析，为Java程序理解提供了综合性的开发辅助工具。

Abstract: Comprendia is an Eclipse plugin that integrates structural dependency visualization with LLM-powered code explanation on a shared interactive graph for Java program comprehension. The tool rests on four pillars: (1) a multi-edge-type dependency graph with live search and multiple layouts; (2) LLM explanations grounded in Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP), an auditable strategy that selects relevant callees using the same graph the developer navigates; (3) a clone-detection overlay that highlights duplication and suggests extract-to-parent refactoring opportunities; and (4) a CVE risk overlay powered by OSV.dev. GACP uses graph distance, inheritance collapse, and edge-type weighting to produce prompts that are reproducible across LLM families and traceable to visible graph nodes. We demonstrate Comprendia on a Java project containing known clones and vulnerabilities, showing how the unified graph substrate supports comprehension while keeping the developer in control. Screencast: https://youtu.be/1wlh_RYehzA

</details>


### [96] [Does the way we write a theory change the program an LLM builds from it? A prospective randomized study of renderer format in LLM theory-to-program translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10314)
*Andre Panossian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过预先注册的随机实验（32个渲染槽位、320个预授权程序），检验大语言模型在将同一理论内容翻译为可执行程序时，是否因呈现格式（结构化vs散文）而产生系统性差异。结果显示两个主要假设均不被支持（NOT_SUPPORTED），同一理论的可识别性接近随机水平（AUC 0.469-0.523），表明渲染格式未产生预期的统一、族不变、可分类的行为几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口头理论无法直接运行，翻译为可执行模型需要在变量、干预和交互方面作出选择。作者希望探究LLM在理论到程序翻译过程中，呈现格式（结构化干预契约 vs 连贯散文）是否会系统性地改变生成程序，从而界定规格格式效应在LLM翻译中的影响边界。

Method: 采用前瞻性预先注册随机实验设计：16个独立分配位将32个配对渲染槽位分配给结构化干预契约和连贯散文；两个固定的LLM快照翻译5个匿名理论账户，生成320个单次程序；使用确定性评估器测量原子有限差分响应（H1）和混合交互响应（H2）；通过精确随机化推断和27项预注册支持标准，在符号线性和幅度秩两条路径上评估两个主要终点（跨模型匹配距离减少和闭集同账户可识别性）。

Result: H1和H2均返回注册判定NOT_SUPPORTED；108项标准评估中仅19项通过；同账户可识别性接近随机水平（AUC 0.469-0.523，注册阈值为0.80）；H2的一个匹配距离终点在符号线性路径中移动并通过多重性校正，但幅度秩结果未达到注册效应量下限，不满足联合支持规则。

Conclusion: 渲染格式未产生预先预测的统一、族不变、可分类的行为几何。该研究为LLM理论到程序翻译中的规格格式效应设定了具体边界，并提供了完全可审计的随机设计、数据集和软件，供可执行形式化研究使用。

Abstract: A verbal theory does not run: translating it into an executable model requires choices about variables, interventions, and interactions. We tested whether the presentation of otherwise identical theoretical content systematically changes the programs produced by large language models. In a prospective preregistered randomized experiment, 16 independent assignment bits allocated 32 paired renderer slots between a structured intervention contract and connected prose. Two pinned LLM snapshots translated five anonymous theoretical accounts, yielding 320 preauthorized single-shot programs in a frozen sparse quadratic language. A deterministic evaluator measured atomic finite-difference responses (H1) and mixed interaction responses (H2). Two primary endpoints assessed cross-model matched-distance reduction and closed-set same-account identifiability, with exact randomization inference and 27 preregistered support criteria evaluated across signed-linear and magnitude-rank pipelines. Both H1 and H2 returned the registered verdict NOT_SUPPORTED; only 19 of 108 criterion evaluations passed. Same-account identifiability remained near chance (AUC 0.469-0.523, against a registered 0.80 threshold). One H2 matched-distance endpoint moved and survived multiplicity correction in the signed-linear pipeline, but the corresponding magnitude-rank result missed the registered effect-size floor, so it did not satisfy the joint support rule. Thus renderer format did not produce the uniform, family-invariant, classifiable behavioral geometry predicted in advance. The result places a concrete boundary on specification-format effects in LLM theory-to-program translation and provides a fully auditable randomized design, datasets, and software for studying executable formalization.

</details>


### [97] [Do Personalized Skills Help Coding Agents? An Empirical Study of Developer Interaction Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10319)
*Shuyan Huang,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了从开发者与LLM代理的交互历史中提取个性化技能以适配开发者偏好的可行性。实验基于13位开发者的206个真实交互会话，发现个性化技能相比无技能基线仅有小幅且不稳定的提升，而跨开发者聚合的通用技能表现最佳。开发者偏好在频繁出现且有多个相关示例时，个性化才更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者与编码代理长期协作中会形成个人偏好，捕获并复用这些偏好可以减少重复纠正、改善协作效率。现有工作主要聚焦于任务特定技能，尚不清楚从交互历史中提取的开发者特定技能能否泛化到未来任务，因此需要系统性研究这一问题。

Method: 提出一个从交互轨迹中提取可复用开发者偏好的框架：首先通过基于规则的bootstrapping和基于证据的refinement生成个性化技能，然后利用可复现的replay框架结合交互式、轨迹条件的LLM人类开发者模拟器进行评估。实验使用206个来自13位开发者的真实会话，对比个性化技能与无技能、通用技能及其他开发者技能等基线。

Result: 个性化技能相比无技能基线仅带来小幅且不一致的提升；跨开发者聚合的通用技能取得了最大且最一致的收益。进一步分析表明，当开发者偏好频繁出现、且历史中包含多个与未来任务相关的示例时，个性化技能才更有效。

Conclusion: 研究为开发者特定个性化何时有效提供了实证洞见，并表明广泛可迁移的程序性知识比开发者特定的偏好信号更为稳健。开发者特定技能的价值取决于偏好出现频率和历史示例的相关性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have rapidly evolved from code-completion tools into solvers of complex software engineering tasks. As developers collaborate with coding agents over time, their preferences emerge through repeated interactions and can be used to adapt agent behavior to better meet individual developers' needs. Capturing and reusing these preferences may reduce repeated corrections and improve developer-agent collaboration. Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for transferring experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing work primarily focuses on task-specific skills, and it remains unclear whether developer-specific skills distilled from interaction histories can generalize to future tasks. We propose a framework for extracting reusable developer preferences from interaction traces. It first generates personalized skills through rule-based bootstrapping and evidence-grounded refinement, and then evaluates them using a reproducible replay framework with an interactive, trajectory-conditioned LLM-based human developer simulator. We conduct an experiment on 206 real-world developer-agent sessions from 13 developers and compare personalized skills against no-skill, generic-skill, and other-user-skill baselines. Personalized skills provide small and inconsistent improvements over the no-skill baseline, whereas generic skills pooled across developers achieve the largest and most consistent gains. Further analysis suggests that personalized skills become more effective when developer preferences appear frequently, particularly when their histories contain multiple examples relevant to future tasks. These findings provide empirical insights into when developer-specific personalization is effective and demonstrate that broadly transferable procedural knowledge can be more robust than developer-specific preference signals.

</details>


### [98] [Persistent Recursive Worlds Enable Autonomous Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10450)
*Beichen Huang,Zhenyu Liang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoX Genesis（简称Genesis），一种以持久化软件项目为核心而非持久化智能体为核心的组织架构。它通过持久递归世界表示软件：有限寿命的智能体仅提出局部变更，递归委派跨路径推进工作，只有被接受的变更才能推进持久版本历史。评估显示Genesis用DeepSeek V4 Flash在120小时内构建了约25万行Rust代码的C编译器（花费仅44美元），通过完整c-testsuite及大部分LLVM和Csmith测试；还实现了13个MESA模块（超10万行Fortran代码到近9万行Rust），在六个数值负载上实现1.55-6.87倍中位加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统的开发周期远超任何单个编码智能体的生命周期。现有智能体系统依赖持久会话、记忆、管理器或共享上下文来维持连续性，作者挑战这一范式，提出应让软件项目本身持久化，而非让智能体持久化。

Method: Genesis将软件表示为持久递归世界：每个局部世界由已接受的版本和仓库路径定位；有限寿命的智能体提出局部变更；递归委派机制在不同路径间移动工作；只有被接受的后果才能推进持久版本历史。评估涵盖三个场景：从零构建C编译器、编译器世界的持续开发与智能体替换、以及MESA数值模块的重实现。

Result: 1) 用DeepSeek V4 Flash从零构建Rust C编译器，约25万行代码，运行120+小时，存档1000+智能体片段，仅花费44美元模型token费用，通过完整c-testsuite和大多数LLVM/Csmith测试。2) 用GLM 5.2生成的编译器世界在反复替换智能体后仍保持完整测试性能。3) 将13个MESA模块（10万+行Fortran）重实现为近9万行Rust工作区，在6个数值负载上实现中位1.55-6.87倍加速。

Conclusion: 长周期软件开发可以围绕持久化项目而非持久化智能体来组织。Genesis证明了有限寿命的局部智能体通过递归委派和版本化世界结构，能够以极低成本完成超大规模、长时程的软件工程项目，为智能体软件工程提供了新的组织范式。

Abstract: Complex software systems develop over timescales that exceed the lifespan of any individual coding agent. Most agentic software systems preserve continuity through persistent sessions, memories, managers or shared context. We introduce EvoX Genesis (hereafter, Genesis), which instead makes the software project persistent while allowing local agents to remain finite-lived. Genesis represents software as a persistent recursive world: each local world is situated by an accepted version and a repository path, finite-lived agents propose local changes, recursive delegation moves work across paths, and only accepted consequences advance the persistent version history. We evaluate this organization across formation, continuation and redevelopment. Starting from a repository with no compiler implementation, Genesis used DeepSeek V4 Flash to build a Rust-based C compiler with about 250k tracked lines; the run lasted over 120 hours, archived over 1,000 agent episodes and incurred only US$44 in model-token charges. The compiler passed the complete c-testsuite and most LLVM and Csmith tests. In a separate compiler world generated with GLM 5.2, development continued after repeated agent replacement while retaining full test performance. Genesis also reimplemented 13 MESA modules with over 100k Fortran lines as a Rust workspace with nearly 90k Rust lines; across six numerical workloads, it achieved median speedups of 1.55--6.87x. These results show that long-horizon software development can be organized around a persistent project rather than a persistent agent.

</details>


### [99] [CausalRepair: Bridging the Causality Gap in Large Language Model-Based Automated Program Repair via Dual-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10613)
*Linhao Wu,Yizhou Chen,Zhen Yang,Pengyu Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CausalRepair框架，通过双切片策略（静态切+动态切）构建最小因果上下文，解决LLM修复程序时的因果缺失问题。在Defects4J等基准上修复313个bug，成本仅$0.029/bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR方法存在"因果鸿沟"：测试上下文可能噪声大或不完整，而静态分析得到的源码上下文常包含无关和未执行代码，误导LLM找不到真正的根因。
