Method: 提出对话驱动的APR框架CausalRepair，采用双切片策略：基于上下文的静态切片净化测试语义，基于执行轨迹的动态切片捕获源码中的精确运行时依赖，共同构建紧凑且因果相关的上下文来引导迭代修复。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2、V2.0和Defects4J-Trans上使用DeepSeek-V3评估，正确修复313个bug，优于ReinFix和TSAPR等SOTA方法，平均修复成本降至$0.029/bug。

Conclusion: 通过最小化因果上下文有效弥合了LLM修复中的因果鸿沟，既提升了修复效果又显著降低了修复成本。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has recently benefited from Large Language Models (LLMs), yet their effectiveness heavily depends on repair context. Existing LLM-based APR methods suffer from a causality gap: test contexts can be noisy or incomplete, while source contexts derived from static analysis often contain irrelevant and unexecuted code, misleading LLMs from identifying the true root cause. To address this issue, we propose CausalRepair, a conversation-driven APR framework based on minimal causal context, i.e., the essential dependencies required to explain a failure. CausalRepair employs a dual-slicing strategy: context-aware static slicing purifies test semantics, while execution-trace-based dynamic slicing captures precise runtime dependencies in source code. Together, they construct compact, causally relevant contexts to guide iterative repair. We evaluate CausalRepair on Defects4J V1.2, V2.0, and Defects4J-Trans using DeepSeek-V3. CausalRepair correctly fixes 313 bugs on Defects4J, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches such as ReinFix and TSAPR, while reducing the average repair cost to $0.029 per bug.

</details>


### [100] [A Study of Cursorrules Files in GitHub Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10622)
*Shuang Sun,Jafar Akhoundali,Arina Kudriavtseva,Sengim Karayalcin,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Cursor中配置文件提示词（.cursorrules）进行了实证研究，收集并分析了来自11,427个GitHub仓库的12,110个.cursorrules文件，发现这类文件从2024年中期开始迅速涌现，主要集中在小型、低活跃度的单人维护仓库中，内容以代码质量和工程实践指导为主，安全相关内容较少，且与当前标准的.mdc文件存在主题连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注对话式提示词（conversational prompts），而配置类提示词文件（prompt files）尚未得到充分研究。随着AI辅助编程的广泛采用，现代工具越来越多地将动态对话提示词与静态配置式提示词文件相结合，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 收集并分析了GitHub上11,427个仓库中的12,110个.cursorrules文件，量化分析其分布、演进和维护情况；同时对65个提示词文件进行定性分析，开发了一个包含65个代码的编码手册，用于捕捉开发者如何表达编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全考虑。

Result: .cursorrules文件从2024年中期开始迅速涌现，其采用集中在小型、低活跃度、单人维护的仓库中，暗示多为玩具项目而非专业开发。提示词文件内容以代码质量、工程实践、项目结构和可维护性为主，安全相关内容出现频率较低。此外，旧的.cursorrules文件与当前标准的.mdc文件在主题和话题上存在连续性。

Conclusion: 配置类提示词文件在AI辅助编程中已形成独特生态，但其当前使用场景以小型项目为主，内容侧重于代码质量和工程实践。安全相关指导的缺乏值得关注，同时.cursorrules到.mdc的演进显示了提示词工程实践的持续发展。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for communicating with AI agents, and they directly influence the quality and reliability of AI-generated code. As AI-assisted programming becomes widely adopted, modern tools increasingly combine dynamic conversational prompts with static configuration-like prompt files. Despite the growing focus on prompt engineering, prior research has primarily focused on conversational prompts, while prompt files remain understudied.
  To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of configuration prompt files in Cursor, a widely used AI-assisted code editor. We collect and analyze over 12,110 .cursorrules files from 11,427 GitHub repositories to characterize their distribution, evolution, and maintenance. Complementing this, we perform qualitative analysis on a random sample of 65 prompt files and develop a 65-code codebook capturing how developers express programming intent, project context, engineering practices, and security considerations.
  Our results show that .cursorrules files emerged rapidly from mid-2024. Their adoption is concentrated in small-scale, low-activity, single-maintainer repositories, suggesting toy projects rather than professional development. The content of prompt files is dominated by guidance on code quality and engineering practices, project structure and configuration, and maintainability, while security-related content appears less frequently. Our analysis shows that there is a continuity of themes and topics between the now-legacy .cursorrules files and the current standard .mdc files.

</details>


### [101] [LLM Ensemble Fault Classification for Automotive HiL Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10710)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的多LLM集成框架，用于汽车HiL验证中的传感器级故障分类。通过紧凑证据表示和异构大语言模型组合（置信度加权投票），在汽油机和电动车HiL系统上，Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B）实现了0.917 Top-1准确率、0.913宏F1和0.902 MCC，优于所有单一模型，并证明模型互补性比集成规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统汽车HiL验证中的多变量测试记录分析依赖人工审查和规则方法，存在效率低、泛化差的问题；现有的机器学习/深度学习故障诊断方法需要大量标注数据、跨工况泛化能力弱，且预测缺乏可解释性，无法满足工程师的诊断需求。

Method: 提出可解释的多LLM集成框架：将故障注入录制的数据转换为紧凑证据表示，融合多个异构大语言模型（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B），采用置信度加权投票策略进行集成决策，在汽油发动机和电动车两个HiL系统、三种驾驶场景、十类单故障条件下进行评估。

Result: 单一模型中Mistral Small 24B最优（Top-1准确率0.903、MCC 0.887、Brier分数最低0.102）；Top-3集成将场景平均结果提升至Top-1准确率0.917、宏F1 0.913、MCC 0.902，且校准性能最佳；Top-5集成未超越Top-3，表明模型互补性优于集成规模。

Conclusion: 协调的多LLM推理能够为汽车HiL验证提供鲁棒、校准良好且工程师可解释的故障分类，多模型集成策略在诊断性能上优于单一模型，且模型间的互补性比单纯增加模型数量更为关键。

Abstract: Automotive HiL validation generates large multivariate test recordings whose analysis remains challenging due to manual review effort, rule-based limitations, and the need for explainable diagnostic decisions. Recent machine-learning and deep-learning approaches have improved fault diagnosis, but they often require large labelled datasets, generalise poorly across operating conditions, and provide limited insight into their predictions. This paper proposes an explainable multi-LLM ensemble framework for sensor-level fault classification in automotive validation. The framework uses compact evidence representations of fault-injection recordings and combines the outputs of heterogeneous large language models to improve diagnostic robustness, ranking quality, confidence reliability, and interpretability. The approach is evaluated on gasoline-engine and electric-vehicle HiL systems across three driving settings and ten single-fault classes. Among the individual models, Mistral Small~24B provides the strongest overall single-model trade-off, achieving 0.903 Top-1 accuracy, 0.887 MCC, and the lowest Brier score of 0.102. The final Top-3 ensemble combines Mistral Small~24B, Qwen2.5~32B, and Phi-4~14B using confidence-weighted voting, improving the scenario-averaged results to 0.917 Top-1 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1, and 0.902 MCC, while also providing the best calibration among the tested ensemble strategies. A Top-5 ensemble does not improve over the Top-3 configuration, indicating that model complementarity is more important than ensemble size. The results show that coordinated multi-LLM reasoning can support robust, calibrated, and engineer-interpretable fault classification for automotive HiL validation.

</details>


### [102] [Conflict Extraction in Probabilistic Datalog Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10755)
*Siyu Chen,Chungha Sung,Xuyang Li,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PPProbe，一种专门针对概率Datalog分析的高效冲突提取器。它利用Datalog推导图的结构特性来定位最小不可满足子集（MUSes），解决概率扩展分析中输出警告虽各自合理但相互不一致的问题。在70个基准测试上，PPProbe比最先进的MUS枚举器快2.5-24倍，平均过滤掉47.7%的互不一致警告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Datalog扩展使静态分析能够按可能性对警报排序，但引入了确定性分析中不存在的新挑战：最终输出中的警报可能各自单独看似合理，却彼此不一致（边际概率不保证联合可满足性），导致开发者可能花费精力调查在任何可能世界中都永远不会共同出现的警报组合。

Method: 本文通过将这种不一致形式化为最小不可满足子集（MUSes）来加以解决，并提出了PPProbe，一个专门针对概率Datalog分析设计的冲突提取器。PPProbe并非改善通用MUS枚举，而是利用Datalog推导图的结构来引导搜索趋向可能的冲突，并通过自底向上的UNSAT推断来剪枝搜索空间。

Result: 在覆盖电源侧信道分析、数据竞争检测、语义差分和贝叶斯网络推断的70个基准测试上，PPProbe比最先进的MUS枚举器实现了2.5到24倍的吞吐量提升，识别出的冲突可平均过滤47.7%的互不一致警报，提供了保守的误报减少估计。

Conclusion: PPProbe通过利用Datalog推导图的领域特定结构，有效解决了概率Datalog分析中警告互不一致的问题，在效率和实用性上都显著优于通用方法，为概率静态分析的实际部署提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Probabilistic extensions of Datalog enable static analyses such as pointer analysis, data race detection, and side-channel analysis to rank alarms by likelihood, but this added expressiveness also introduces a new challenge absent from deterministic analyses: the final output may contain alarms that are individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent, because marginal probabilities do not guarantee joint satisfiability. As a result, developers may spend effort investigating combinations of alarms that can never co-occur in any possible world. We address this problem by formalizing such inconsistencies as minimal unsatisfiable subsets (MUSes) and introducing PPProbe, a conflict extractor specialized for probabilistic Datalog analyses. Rather than improving MUS enumeration in general, PPProbe exploits the structure of Datalog derivation graphs to guide the search toward likely conflicts and prune the search space through bottom-up UNSAT inference. We evaluate PPProbe on 70 benchmarks from power side-channel analysis, data race detection, semantic diffing, and Bayesian-network inference. The results show that PPProbe achieves 2.5 to 24 times higher throughput than state-of-the-art MUS enumerators, and that the conflicts it identifies yield a conservative estimate of false-positive reduction, filtering out an average of 47.7% of mutually inconsistent alarms.

</details>


### [103] [Extending Triple Graph Grammars to Formalize Complex View Definitions on Families of Models - Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10785)
*Lars König,Jens Kosiol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文为NeoJoin视图定义语言赋予基于三重图文法（TGGs）的形式语义，并引入skip语义来解决重叠查询翻译的支持缺口，从而实现模型与视图间的自动化、增量式同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视图的开发需要视图定义语言既易于使用又具有精确形式语义，以支持多视图工作的受控集成和系统级一致性。NeoJoin语言之前的工作需要完善其形式语义框架，同时现有TGG方法在支持重叠查询翻译方面存在不足。

Method: 延续此前工作，为NeoJoin视图定义语言建立基于三重图文法（TGGs）的形式语义；识别出重叠查询翻译支持这一关键缺口后，引入TGG规则的skip语义——允许根据应用上下文跳过规则规定的某些动作。

Result: 通过skip语义填补了NeoJoin视图定义语言在重叠查询翻译方面的缺口，获得了具备高度形式保证的自动化增量同步程序，同时也进一步验证了TGG的表述能力和可用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为NeoJoin赋予形式化基础，支持模型与视图间高保证的自动化同步，同时通过skip语义扩展丰富了TGG的形式语义，为TGG作为视图定义语言语义基础的适用性提供了又一案例验证。

Abstract: View-based development is an important technique to manage complexity and facilitate cooperation in the development of modern and complex systems of systems. This approach places high demands on view definition languages used in the development process. First, such a language needs to be approachable and extremely versatile so that developers from diverse backgrounds are able to use it and can define views for a diverse set of different tasks. Yet, the language also needs to have a precise and formal semantics so that it is possible to integrate the work performed on various views in a controlled manner to obtain a coherent overall system. In this paper, we continue previous work of equipping the NeoJoin view definition language with a formal semantics that is based on triple graph grammars (TGGs). This paves the way for obtaining automated and incremental synchronization procedures between models and views that come with high formal guarantees for their behavior. Simultaneously, our work serves as a further case study of the expressivity and usability of TGGs. We identify one gap, namely convenient support for the translation of overlapping queries from the view definition language, and tackle that gap by introducing a skip semantics for TGG rules, i.e., allowing to skip certain actions a rule prescribes, depending on the application context.

</details>


### [104] [From Pattern Detection to Composition Analysis in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10882)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Erico Augusto da Silva,Anthony Accioly,Higor Amario de Souza,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了量子软件模式挖掘工具，新增两个匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤，在Qrisp框架上达到0.712的micro-F1（未扩展时为0.449），并构建组合图存入图数据库以分析模式实现如何组装及共现原因，最终发布了开源挖掘流水线qpa及知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件模式提供了构建量子程序的高级抽象，但缺乏对其在实际中采用情况的实证证据。此前工作已建立61个模式的目录和知识库，本文旨在提升模式挖掘工具的准确性，并深入分析模式实现之间的组装关系与共现原因。

Method: 1) 在原有工具基础上扩展两个匹配通道并增加词汇扩展步骤；2) 在未包含于知识库的Qrisp框架上执行定量评估；3) 构建记录模式相关高层框架组件间调用关系的组合图，并存入图数据库；4) 利用LLM集成对新提取组件进行分类，维护知识库（覆盖5个来源、286个框架组件）。

Result: 在Qrisp框架上达到micro-F1为0.712，而不使用词汇扩展时为0.449，显著提升了挖掘准确性。所有23个模式均在80个项目中实际出现。

Conclusion: 作者发布了qpa开源挖掘流水线、知识库（286个组件映射到模式目录）以及模式使用数据集，支持量子模式采用与演化的可复现研究。

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>


### [105] [GitSkills: A Dataset of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10906)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Daniel Graziotin,Matteo Vaccargiu,Marco Ortu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GitSkills——一个包含约380万个SKILL.md文件（来自28.2万个公共仓库）的agent技能数据集，提供SQLite格式存储，用于研究agent技能的采用、复用、结构、作者、维护和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能（含SKILL.md文件的文件夹）是新兴的软件工件，但没有中央注册表或包管理器，只能通过复制文件夹在仓库间传播。如何在实践中编写、复用和维护技能缺乏实证数据，也没有现成数据集记录这一群体，因此需要构建专门的数据集来填补这一空白。

Method: 从2026年7月收集了282,200个公共仓库中的3,797,117个SKILL.md文件，保留每个文件的仓库、路径和内容哈希；将相同文件分组为1,877,981个不同内容，并对每个组的代表文件进行富化，包括全文、解析的front matter、文件夹内容、仓库元数据以及部分文件的提交历史；最终以单个自包含SQLite文件形式发布。

Result: 构建了包含379万+SKILL.md文件、282,200个仓库、约188万个不同内容的GitSkills数据集，并以自包含SQLite文件形式提供给研究社区。

Conclusion: GitSkills为研究agent技能的采用、复用、结构、作者归属、维护和安全性提供了首个大规模实证数据集，填补了该领域缺乏数据支撑的空白。

Abstract: An agent skill is a folder containing a SKILL.md file with instructions for a language-model agent, optionally accompanied by scripts and reference files. The agent loads the skill when it judges that a task matches the skill description. Anthropic introduced the format in October 2025 as an open specification. Nine months later, we find that skill files in the millions sit in public GitHub repositories. Skills are unlike the artifacts the SE research community usually mines: they are written mainly in natural language, a model selects them probabilistically at run time, and no compiler or type checker verifies the selection. They also have no central registry or package manager, so they spread by copying folders between repositories. How developers write, reuse, and maintain skills is therefore an empirical question, and no existing dataset records this population. We present GitSkills, a dataset of 3,797,117 SKILL.md files collected from 282,200 public repositories in July 2026. The dataset retains every file occurrence with its repository, path, and content hash. It groups identical files into 1,877,981 distinct contents and enriches one representative per group with the full text, parsed front matter, folder contents, repository metadata, and, for a subset, the commit history of the file. A single self- contained SQLite file supports research on the adoption, reuse, structure, authorship, maintenance, and security of agent skills.

</details>


### [106] [Understanding the Architecture of Coding Agents: An Exploratory Study Using a Research Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10934)
*Marco Tulio Valente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地记录了编码代理（coding agents）的主要架构组件，并提出了Arg、一个极简开源编码代理及其轻量级基准ArkBench，用于教育和研究目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管编码代理已成为AI辅助软件开发的主要界面，但其内部架构鲜有系统性描述，缺乏类似编译器或操作系统那样成熟的架构文档，这一空白亟需填补。

Method: 作者设计并实现了Arg（Agent Research Kit），一个保留现代编码代理核心架构机制但强调简洁清晰的开源代理；同时提出ArkBench，包含10个软件维护与演化任务的轻量级基准，并使用gpt-5.4-mini模型进行实验验证。

Result: Arg在ArkBench的10个任务中成功解决了8个，同时token消耗适中；此外，作者还利用近期提出的架构分类法，将Arg与最先进的编码代理进行了架构对比。

Conclusion: Arg和ArkBench为编码代理的教学、研究与实验提供了实用的基础，有望推动该领域架构知识的系统化积累。

Abstract: Coding agents have rapidly emerged as the primary interface for AI-assisted software development. However, despite their growing adoption, relatively little is known about their internal architecture, and no systematic architectural description comparable to those available for compilers or operating systems currently exists. This paper addresses this gap by documenting the main architectural components of coding agents, explaining their responsibilities, interactions, and execution flow. To support this effort, we also present Ark (Agent Research Kit), a minimal open-source coding agent designed for research and education that preserves the essential architectural mechanisms of modern coding agents while emphasizing simplicity and clarity. We also introduce ArkBench, a lightweight benchmark comprising ten representative software maintenance and evolution tasks. Using gpt-5.4-mini, Ark successfully solved 8 of the 10 tasks while requiring modest token consumption. Finally, we compare the architecture of Ark with those of state-of-the-art coding agents using a recently proposed architectural taxonomy. We hope that both Ark and ArkBench provide a practical foundation for teaching, research, and experimentation on coding agents.

</details>


### [107] [Agentic Configuration Management (ACM): A Reference Configuration Model for Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11166)
*Audrey Quessada-Vial*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic配置管理（ACM），一个框架无关的治理与配置参考模型，用于异构智能体系统。ACM通过类型化配置项、不可变修订与基线、配置-运行时分离、生命周期与保障语义、依赖感知影响传播及运行时溯源，将异构原生配置通过语义投影规范化为规范配置图，提供统一治理。Python参考实现支持LangGraph、CrewAI和OpenAI Agents SDK，经27个治理场景和9个量化影响传播案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMOps和AgentOps平台虽支持编排与可观测性，但缺乏统一的配置治理模型来将智能体系统表示为连贯的、版本化的配置，难以实现跨框架的治理、可复现性与审计。

Method: 提出ACM参考模型，包含：类型化且独立版本化的Agentic Configuration Items、不可变修订与基线、显式配置-运行时分离、生命周期与保障语义、依赖感知的影响传播、运行时溯源；通过语义投影将异构原生配置规范化为规范配置图，并在其上操作统一治理语义；影响语义形式化为有限格上的单调传播，建立收敛性、终止性及最小不动点唯一性。

Result: 提供Python参考实现及三个框架适配器，在评估的配置范围内三个框架产生治理等价且可复现的ACM表示；27个治理场景和9个量化影响传播案例验证了可复现性、可审计性、依赖分析和互操作性。

Conclusion: 结果表明通用治理语义能够在评估范围内支持跨异构智能体执行抽象的复制、审计、依赖分析和互操作性，为Agentic系统配置治理奠定了理论基础和实证基础。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly composed of heterogeneous agents, prompts, tools, models, skills, composite subsystems, policies, and execution workflows whose configurations evolve across frameworks and runtime environments. Existing LLMOps and AgentOps platforms support orchestration and observability but do not provide a common configuration-governance model for representing and governing these systems as coherent, versioned configurations.
  This paper introduces Agentic Configuration Management (ACM), a framework-independent governance and configuration reference model for heterogeneous agentic systems. ACM combines typed and independently versioned Agentic Configuration Items, immutable revisions and baselines, explicit configuration-runtime separation, lifecycle and assurance semantics, dependency-aware impact propagation, and runtime provenance. Heterogeneous native configurations are normalized through semantic projection into a canonical Configuration Graph on which common governance semantics operate.
  We provide a Python reference implementation with adapters for LangGraph, CrewAI, and the OpenAI Agents SDK. The evaluation combines 27 governance scenarios with nine quantitative impact-propagation cases. For the evaluated configurations, the three frameworks yield governance-equivalent ACM representations and reproducible governance outcomes after projection. The impact semantics are formalized as monotone propagation over a finite lattice, establishing convergence, termination, and uniqueness of the least fixed point above the initial impact valuation. These results provide evidence that common governance semantics can support reproducibility, auditability, dependency analysis, and interoperability across heterogeneous agentic execution abstractions within the evaluated scope.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Sequential Modality Dropout for Robust Multi-Modal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10240)
*Guanqun Yang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出顺序模态丢弃（SMD）方法，在训练时以概率p独立擦除图像或文本模态的整个用户交互历史，使多模态顺序推荐模型学会在缺少任一模态时仍能准确预测下一项，实现在实际部署中模态缺失情况下的鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实产品目录中经常缺失图像或文本等信息，但现有的多模态顺序推荐模型假设每个条目都携带所有模态；当服务阶段某个模态不可用时，在完整数据上训练的模型推荐精度会大幅下降，现有方法缺乏应对此问题的能力。

Method: 在训练阶段，对每个模态流（图像和文本）以概率p独立地对整个用户交互历史进行擦除，强制模型在不依赖任何单一模态的情况下学习预测下一项；此外还提出可选的跨模态重建损失进一步增强鲁棒性。该方法仅需四行代码改动，且架构无关。

Result: 在四个骨干模型（MM-SASRec、IISAN、MISSRec、fMRLRec）和四个Amazon域上，SMD将文本保留率提升1.0至3.2倍而对完整模态精度几乎无损失；在极端95%逐项缺失率下，可保留HR@10的61%（对比无SMD时的22%，提升2.8倍）；可选的跨模态重建损失在严重文本缺失下将保留率从90%提升至98%。

Conclusion: SMD作为一个轻量、架构无关的训练改动，能有效提升多模态顺序推荐系统在实际部署中面对模态缺失时的鲁棒性，以极小的代价获得显著的抗缺失能力提升，具有广泛适用价值。

Abstract: Multi-modal sequential recommenders assume every item carries every modality, but real product catalogs often miss images or text, and a model trained on complete data loses much of its recommendation accuracy when a modality is unavailable at serving time. We propose Sequential Modality Dropout (SMD): during training, each modality stream (image and text) is independently erased with probability p for an entire user interaction history, so the model learns to predict the next item without relying on any single modality. We measure robustness by retention, the fraction of a model's full-modality accuracy (HR@10) that survives when a modality is removed at test time. Across four backbones (MM-SASRec, IISAN, MISSRec, and fMRLRec) on four Amazon domains, SMD raises text retention by 1.0 to 3.2x at essentially no cost to full-modality accuracy; under an extreme 95% per-item missing rate, it retains 61% of HR@10 versus 22% without (a 2.8x improvement). An optional cross-modal reconstruction loss further lifts retention from 90% to 98% on a simple additive backbone under severe text missingness. SMD is a four-line, architecture-agnostic change that makes multi-modal sequential recommenders robust to the missing modalities they actually encounter in deployment.

</details>


### [109] [Do LLM Recommenders Know When They're Hallucinating? Auditing Confidence Calibration in Catalog Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10008)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对四个零样本LLM推荐系统（Mistral Large、Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Sonnet 4.6）在三个目录（MovieLens-25M、Amazon Toys、Yelp）上的幻觉率和口头化置信度校准进行了联合审计。发现幻觉率因目录而异（0–8.4%），但所有模型在所有单元格中系统性地表现出置信不足（而非通常强调的过度自信），ECE最高达0.223。口头化置信度无法区分正确项与幻觉项，导致共形弃权阈值效果有限。建议审计应同时报告校准与OOD，并采用目录锚定的提示方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM推荐器的审计仅将幻觉视为二元的目录外（OOD）率，没有检验模型是否知道自己产生了幻觉。作者旨在联合审计幻觉率与置信度校准，以揭示两者之间的关系，并检验口头化置信度能否用于降低幻觉。

Method: 对四个来自独立厂商的零样本LLM推荐器，在三个目录上按物品流行度分层，测量OOD@10幻觉率和口头化置信度的校准指标（ECE、Brier、可靠性），并测试基于置信度的共形弃权阈值用于降低幻觉的效果。

Result: 幻觉率因目录而异（MovieLens上0–0.2%，Amazon上4.5–8.3%，Yelp上2.2–8.4%），但即使在零幻觉情况下置信度也严重失准（MovieLens上ECE高达0.223）。所有四个LLM在所有十二个单元格中系统性地置信不足：推荐准确率达92–100%的项平均置信度仅67–86。共形弃权阈值最多只能将幻觉降低0.7个百分点，却需付出4–21个百分点的覆盖率代价。

Conclusion: 口头化置信度反映的是通用的推荐质量评分，而非目录成员概率（引出失配）。审计应同时报告校准与OOD，并使用目录锚定的引出方式而非通用置信度提示。置信不足通道无法分离正确项与幻觉项，因此仅靠口头化置信度无法有效缓解幻觉。

Abstract: LLM recommenders for top-$K$ item suggestion regularly emit titles outside the target catalog. Prior audits measure this as a binary out-of-domain rate; none ask whether the model knew it was hallucinating. We jointly audit hallucination rate (OOD@10) and verbalized-confidence calibration (ECE, Brier, reliability) for four zero-shot LLM recommenders from four independent vendors (Mistral Large, Llama-3.3-70B, GPT-OSS-120B, Claude Sonnet 4.6), not grounded or fine-tuned systems, across three catalogs (MovieLens-25M, Amazon Reviews 2023 Toys, Yelp Open Dataset), stratified by item popularity. Hallucination is catalog-dependent (0--0.2\% on MovieLens, 4.5--8.3\% on Amazon, 2.2--8.4\% on Yelp), but verbalized confidence is materially miscalibrated even when hallucination is zero (ECE up to 0.223 on MovieLens despite 0\% OOD). All four LLMs are systematically \emph{under}-confident across all twelve cells, verbalizing a mean of 67--86 on items they recommend with 92--100\% accuracy. This is the opposite of the over-confidence usually emphasized in LLM-hallucination work. The under-confidence is best read as an \emph{elicitation mismatch}: ``Just Ask'' elicits a generic recommendation-quality rating, not a catalog-membership probability. A conformal abstention threshold over verbalized confidence reduces hallucination by at most 0.7\,pp across $α\in \{.05, .10, .15, .20\}$, at 4--21\,pp of coverage cost: the under-confident channel cannot separate correct items from hallucinations, so the threshold mostly removes correct items. We recommend that audits of LLM recommenders report calibration alongside OOD, and use catalog-anchored elicitation rather than generic confidence prompts.

</details>


### [110] [ConnectionMind: Leveraging Social Networks and Large Language Models for Personalized Recommendation at Meta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10187)
*Haoyu Han,Yuming Liu,Lei Huang,Lizhu Zhang,Jiliang Tang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ConnectionMind是Meta开发的一个生产级推荐框架，通过将社交网络结构与大型语言模型（LLM）紧密集成，将推荐问题建模为异构图上的路径推理问题。它采用两阶段训练策略（SFT+RL），已在Meta的大规模推荐管线中部署，在线A/B测试实现视频观看时长提升0.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代社交媒体平台（如Meta）的推荐系统需要同时建模复杂的社交关系（好友、群组、创作者互动）和异构内容（文本、视频），但传统推荐模型往往忽略这些信号或将其独立处理，缺乏整合多关系上下文进行细粒度个性化的推理能力。

Method: ConnectionMind构建了一个连接用户、物品、好友、群组和创作者页面的异构图，将推荐形式化为从用户到候选物品的个性化路径发现的图推理问题。采用基于LLM的策略对图结构进行推理并指导推荐决策。训练上采用两阶段策略：先在大规模用户-物品交互轨迹上进行监督微调（SFT）初始化推理策略，再用端到端强化学习（RL）优化模型在图上的推理能力。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，ConnectionMind相比代表性基线方法表现更优。更重要的是，该系统已在Meta的大规模推荐管线中部署，并通过在线A/B测试评估，视频观看时长提升了0.43%。

Conclusion: ConnectionMind证明了将社交图结构与LLM推理能力结合的推荐框架在生产环境中具有可扩展性和实际价值，实现了可解释、推理感知的个性化推荐，并在真实生产系统中取得了可衡量的业务影响。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems on social media platforms such as Meta must model complex social relationships, including friendships, group memberships, and creator interactions, alongside massive and heterogeneous content such as text and video. Traditional recommendation models, however, often omit these signals or treat them independently, lacking the reasoning capability to integrate multi-relational context for fine-grained personalization. We present ConnectionMind, a production-ready recommendation framework that tightly integrates the social network structure with large language models (LLMs) to enable scalable, interpretable, and reasoning-aware personalization in Meta. ConnectionMind constructs a heterogeneous graph connecting users, items, friends, groups, and creator pages, and formulates recommendation as a graph reasoning problem: discovering personalized paths from users to candidate items. An LLM-based policy is employed to reason over these graph structures and guide recommendation decisions. To train the system at scale, ConnectionMind adopts a two-stage learning strategy. We first perform supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on large-scale user-item interaction trajectories to initialize the reasoning policy, followed by end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) to refine the model's ability to reason over social graphs for personalized recommendation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ConnectionMind compared to representative baselines. More importantly, ConnectionMind has been deployed in Meta's large-scale recommendation pipeline and has been evaluated through online A/B tests, achieving a 0.43% improvement in video watch time. These results demonstrate measurable real-world impact in a production recommendation system.

</details>


### [111] [DualSpectralCF: Training-Free Sign-Aware Spectral Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10247)
*Guanqun Yang,Tong Qi,Xiaoxue Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DualSpectralCF是一个免训练的符号感知协同过滤框架，通过有符号输入信号和有符号物品-物品算子桥接训练自由频谱方法与显式负反馈建模，在五个基准数据集上匹配或超越无符号基线，并大幅提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实推荐平台收集大量显式负反馈（如1星评分、踩按钮等），但现有的符号感知推荐器依赖梯度训练，成本高；而免训练的频谱协同过滤方法虽然高效，却只处理正交互信号。作者希望弥合这两类方法之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出DualSpectralCF框架，包含两个组件：有符号输入信号r_u^±编码用户显式厌恶项，以及有符号物品-物品算子M^±融合"同类喜欢"和"同类厌恶"相似性。框架与骨干无关，仅增加两个标量超参数，可嵌入ChebyCF、GF-CF和Turbo-CF等频谱骨干。

Result: 在五个符号感知基准上，每个实例在所有5个数据集上均匹配或超越无符号骨干，Recall@20提升最高达+32.6%；固定默认超参数下提升+1.9%至+16.0%；运行速度比SIGformer快7.7至155.3倍，并达到其70.7%至90.7%的精度。对冷启动用户提升最显著，在Epinions上1-5个训练物品的用户Recall@20提升达+29.2%。

Conclusion: DualSpectralCF证明了显式负反馈可以在免训练的频谱框架中有效利用，无需梯度训练即可获得显著的精度提升，尤其在冷启动场景下表现突出，为符号感知推荐提供了高效且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Real-world recommendation platforms routinely collect explicit negative feedback such as 1-star reviews, hate-button clicks, distrust between users, and very-low watch-ratio videos. Learned sign-aware recommenders exploit this signal for clear accuracy gains, but only at the cost of gradient-based training. In parallel, a line of training-free spectral collaborative filtering methods matches or beats learned graph recommenders at a fraction of the cost, yet operates on positive interactions alone. We bridge these two lines with DualSpectralCF, a training-free framework of two components that attach to any spectral backbone of the form $\hat{\mathbf{r}}_u = F(\mathbf{M}) \mathbf{r}_u$: a signed input signal $\mathbf{r}_u^{\pm}$ that encodes the user's explicit dislikes, and a signed item-item operator $\mathbf{M}^{\pm}$ that blends like-together and dislike-together similarity. The framework is backbone-agnostic and adds just two scalar hyperparameters. We instantiate DualSpectralCF on ChebyCF, GF-CF, and Turbo-CF, and evaluate on five sign-aware benchmarks: every instance matches or beats its unsigned backbone on all 5 datasets, with Recall@20 lifts up to +32.6% with backbone-specific $(γ, κ)$ tuning and +1.9% to +16.0% for DualSpectralCF-Cheby at the fixed default $(γ= -0.5, κ= 0.1)$, and the family runs 7.7 to 155.3$\times$ faster than SIGformer while reaching 70.7% to 90.7% of its accuracy. Sign-awareness helps most for cold-start users, with up to +29.2% Recall@20 on Epinions users with 1 to 5 training items.

</details>


### [112] [GenRec: An LLM-Backed Recommendation Ranker at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10257)
*Ying Li,Shradha Sehgal,Arjun Rao,Rein Houthooft,Yaochen Zhu,Ashish Rastogi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Netflix提出GenRec，一种基于大语言模型的推荐排序器，采用两阶段框架：第一阶段将开源LLM适配到Netflix数据，第二阶段进行推荐排序特定的后训练。通过大规模A/B测试证明，GenRec在显著减少标签训练样本和输入信号的情况下，仍能在离线和在线指标上取得统计学显著提升，并展示了从特征工程到上下文工程、从定制架构到共享基础模型的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的判别式推荐排序器依赖数千个人工设计的特征，建模和维护成本高，且难以充分利用自然语言中丰富的用户、内容和上下文信息。作者希望通过LLM的能力重塑推荐系统，探索一种更为通用和高效的推荐范式。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：Phase 1将开源LLM适应到Netflix数据，建立对目录和用户行为的深度理解；Phase 2使用推荐排序特定的数据、标签和奖励信号进行后训练，使排序器与业务需求和用户长期满意度对齐。具体包括输入语言化和上下文工程、后训练数据构建、奖励整合、模型架构设计，以及基于prefill-only推理路径的成本约束服务方案。

Result: 在大型A/B测试中，GenRec以显著更少的Phase 2标注训练样本和输入信号，在离线和在线指标上均取得了较当前生产排序器的统计学显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的推荐系统有望将推荐范式从特征工程转向上下文工程，从定制化架构转向共享基础模型骨架。同时，在实际资源约束下服务此类系统需要务实的设计取舍，文中也给出了相应的实践经验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are reshaping recommender systems by enabling richer modeling of users, content, and context directly in natural language. At Netflix, we are exploring this direction through GenRec, an LLM-backed recommendation ranker built on top of an in-house foundational LLM. GenRec follows a two-phase framework: Phase 1 adapts an open-source LLM to Netflix data, developing deep understanding of the catalog and member behavior while balancing capabilities such as content understanding and instruction following. Phase 2 post-trains this foundation model with recommendation-ranking specific data, labels, and reward signals, aiming to align the ranker with business requirements and long-term member satisfaction.
  This paper focuses on Phase 2 and the transition from a traditional discriminative ranker with thousands of engineered features to an LLM-backed ranker driven by verbalized user histories and context. We describe our design for input verbalization and context engineering, post-training data construction, reward integration, model architecture, and a cost-constrained serving design based on a prefill-only inference approach. We report results from a large-scale A/B test comparing GenRec against the current production ranker model, where we show that a GenRec model trained with substantially fewer Phase-2 labeled training examples and input signals can achieve statistically significant gains in offline and online metrics. We discuss how LLM-backed recommenders could shift the recommendation paradigm: from feature engineering to context engineering, and from bespoke architectures to shared foundation backbones. We also outline practical lessons for serving such systems under real-world resource constraints.

</details>


### [113] [Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering: Rethinking Graph Collaborative Filtering as Tree Collaborative Filtering with Curvature-Aware Propagation Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10297)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出NTCF框架，从树结构视角重新审视图协同过滤，根据节点局部度不平衡分数（作为离散Ricci曲率代理）为每个节点分配自适应的传播深度，以解决统一传播深度导致的外围节点过平滑和中心节点探索不足问题，理论证明其是NGCF的严格泛化，实验表明优于多数主流GCF骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图协同过滤(GCF)对所有节点使用统一的传播深度，但真实交互图中节点局部连通性差异显著：外围节点容易遭受过平滑问题，而中心节点在直接邻域之外探索不足。这种"一刀切"的传播策略忽略了节点间结构异质性。

Method: 将每个节点的局部邻域重新解释为有根树，基于闭式局部度不平衡分数（作为离散Ricci曲率代理）为每个节点分配特定传播深度。提供理论分析证明：(i)NTCF严格泛化NGCF，当曲率引起的深度调整消失时退化为NGCF；(ii)曲率感知调度在正曲率（外围）节点深层保留更多判别性信息。

Result: NTCF在三个公开数据集上优于大多数广泛使用的GCF骨干模型，并可替代现有先进自监督模型的骨干模块以提升其性能。

Conclusion: 曲率感知的节点特定传播深度能够有效克服统一深度传播的局限，NTCF既可作为独立模型也可作为增强骨干集成到现有系统中，验证了从树结构视角建模GCF的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Graph Collaborative Filtering (GCF) has become the dominant paradigm in modern recommender systems by modeling user-item interactions as a bipartite graph and propagating embeddings through a fixed number of message-passing layers. However, applying a uniform propagation depth to every node ignores a fundamental property of real interaction graphs: nodes differ substantially in their local connectivity, so peripheral nodes quickly suffer from over-smoothing while hub-like nodes remain under-explored beyond their immediate neighborhood. In this paper, we revisit GCF from a tree-structured perspective and propose Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering (NTCF), a framework that re-interprets each node's local neighborhood as a rooted tree and assigns a node-specific propagation depth based on a closed-form local-degree-imbalance score that serves as a discrete Ricci-curvature proxy. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that (i) NTCF strictly generalizes NGCF, degenerating to NGCF when all curvature-induced depth adjustments vanish (a lower bound on its representation power), and (ii) the curvature-aware schedule retains strictly more discriminative information at deep layers on positively-curved (peripheral) nodes than uniform-depth propagation. NTCF can achieve higher performance than most widely used GCF backbone models and can be integrated into existing advanced self-supervised models as a backbone, replacing their original backbone to achieve enhanced performance. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of NTCF.

</details>


### [114] [Persona Conditioning as an Assessor-Sensitivity Probe for LLM-Based IR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10385)
*Samaneh Mohtadi,Pietro Bernardelle,Joel Mackenzie,Gianluca Demartini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为相关性评估者时，人物角色条件化（persona conditioning）如何影响判断可靠性和系统排名比较。通过在两个数据集上使用六种LLM骨干模型和五种评估者角色进行实验，发现评估者对角色敏感是结构化的而非均匀的：判断通常接近基线但会改变严格程度和证据阈值，高容量模型保持系统排名一致性而小模型放大不稳定性，且敏感性集中在特定检索系统类型上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作IR评估中的相关性评估者，但关于评估者框架如何影响判断可靠性和下游系统比较的问题尚不清楚。作者希望通过人物角色条件化作为诊断机制，暴露LLM评估者的敏感性，从而识别出评估结果容易受评估者框架影响的系统。

Method: 使用来自PersonaHub和NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA两个互补来源的任务导向人物角色，实例化五种评估者角色（意图解释、领域专长、对比判断、证据验证、全局搜索质量评估），与标准UMBRELA基线对比。在六个LLM骨干模型上，使用TREC DL20和RAG24数据集进行评估实验，并进行局部排名位移分析。

Result: 发现评估者对角色敏感是结构化的：判断通常接近基线但改变严格程度、证据阈值或解释重点，而非产生广泛的相关性反转。系统层面上，高容量模型保持系统排名一致性，而小模型放大角色诱导的不稳定性。敏感性集中在特定检索系统类型上：DL20上集中于神经排序/重排序系统，RAG24上集中于RAG导向流水线。人物角色来源的影响小于评估者角色和模型容量。

Conclusion: 角色条件化评估可作为受控的敏感性探针，用于压力测试基于LLM的IR评估流水线，并识别出评估结果对评估者框架敏感的系统。这为理解LLM评估者的可靠性和局限性提供了结构性视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as relevance assessors in information retrieval (IR) evaluation, raising questions about how assessor framing affects judgment reliability and downstream system comparison. We study persona conditioning as a diagnostic mechanism for exposing LLM assessor sensitivity. Using task-oriented personas drawn from two complementary sources (PersonaHub and NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA), we instantiate five assessor roles emphasizing intent interpretation, domain expertise, contrastive judgment, evidence verification, and global search-quality assessment, compared with a standard UMBRELA baseline. Across six LLM backbones on TREC DL20 and RAG24, our analyses reveal structured rather than uniform assessor sensitivity. Judgments usually remain close to the baseline while shifting assessment strictness, evidential threshold, or interpretation emphasis rather than producing widespread relevance reversals. At the system level, high-capacity models preserve system-ranking agreement, while smaller models amplify persona-induced instability. Local rank-displacement analysis shows sensitivity concentrates on particular retrieval systems and system types, especially neural ranking/reranking systems on DL20 and RAG-oriented pipelines on RAG24. Persona source matters less than assessor role and model capacity. These findings position persona-conditioned judging as a controlled sensitivity probe for stress-testing LLM-based IR evaluation pipelines and identifying systems whose evaluation outcomes are sensitive to assessor framing.

</details>


### [115] [Post-Calibration Reliability Reranking of Relevance Decisions via Label-wise Monotone Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10406)
*Inwoo Tae,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出标签级单调可靠性投影（MRP）方法，通过学习标签级单调函数将校准后的置信度映射为正确性可靠性分数，在保留原始预测标签和类别概率的同时对固定预测进行重排序，解决了传统校准仅对齐平均正确率而无法消除同一校准置信度内依赖于预测标签的可靠性差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后验校准仅使置信度与平均正确率对齐，但未消除同一校准置信度水平内因预测标签不同而残留的可靠性差异，导致系统可能过度信任错误预测或不必要地推迟正确预测。

Method: 提出标签级单调可靠性投影（MRP），学习每个标签对应的单调函数，将校准后的置信度映射为正确性可靠性，同时保留原始预测标签和类别概率，并根据残差风险对固定预测进行重排序。

Result: 在六个信息访问相关性数据集和多种后验校准器上，MRP提升了可靠性重排序和平均回退效用，同时保持全量覆盖的准确率和期望校准误差（ECE）。结构消融表明主要收益来自标签级残差可靠性而非全局置信度重映射。

Conclusion: MRP通过标签级单调映射有效补充了传统校准的不足，在信息访问检索系统中显著改善可靠性重排序和回退决策；同时分析了将可靠性分数嵌入顶层标签概率几何的兼容性，并表明该投影作为兼容性分析有用但不同于主要可靠性重排序目标。

Abstract: Web search, product search, and question-answering retrieval systems often assign a relevance label and confidence score to each query-candidate pair. The relevance label describes how well a page, product, or passage matches the query, while the confidence often guides downstream use or fallback decisions. Post-hoc calibration is therefore needed because misaligned confidence can make systems over-trust wrong predictions or unnecessarily defer correct ones. However, calibration mainly aligns confidence with average correctness, and does not remove predicted-label-dependent reliability differences that remain within the same calibrated confidence level. We address this gap with Label-wise Monotone Reliability Projection (MRP), which learns label-wise monotone functions that map calibrated confidence to correctness reliability while preserving the original predicted labels and class probabilities. The resulting reliability score reranks fixed predictions according to residual risk. Across six information access relevance datasets and multiple post-hoc calibrators, MRP improves reliability reranking and average fallback utility while preserving full-coverage accuracy and ECE. Structural ablations show that the main gains come from label-wise residual reliability rather than from global confidence remapping. We further analyze when MRP reliability scores can be embedded back into top-label probability geometry, showing that this projection is useful as a compatibility analysis but is distinct from the main reliability-reranking objective. The implementation will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Efficient Reasoning in LLM-Based Recommender Systems via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10447)
*Linh Dieu Le,Tong Chen,Shazia Sadiq,Hongzhi Yin,Ming Jin,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出REAM，首个面向推荐系统推理压缩的模型合并框架。不同于传统均匀合并方法，REAM在注意力头级别进行细粒度合并，为每个注意力头分配不同的合并系数，选择性将快思考模型的简洁行为注入慢思考模型，在三个基准数据集上将推理长度减少最多24.3%，同时保持推荐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的推荐系统越来越多采用慢思考模型，通过逐步推理获得更高准确率，但推理轨迹冗长导致推理成本高。现有训练式压缩方法适应成本高，推理时方法脆弱且难以扩展，亟需一种训练免费、可扩展的推理压缩方法。

Method: 提出REAM框架，采用模型合并方式在共享参数空间中迁移慢思考模型与快思考模型的行为。与传统合并方法使用均匀合并系数不同，REAM在单个注意力头级别进行细粒度合并，根据每个注意力头对关键推理证据的贡献及其对参数变化的敏感性，分配不同的合并系数，实现选择性注入快思考模型的简洁行为。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，REAM将推理长度减少最多24.3%，同时在保持推荐准确性方面优于竞争性的模型合并基线方法。

Conclusion: REAM验证了注意力头级别细粒度模型合并作为推荐系统推理压缩的有效性，提供了一种训练免费且可扩展的解决方案，在推理简洁性和推荐准确性之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language model-based recommender systems are increasingly adopting slow-thinking models that generate step-by-step reasoning before making predictions, often achieving higher accuracy than fast-thinking models that predict directly. However, their reasoning traces are often unnecessarily verbose, increasing inference costs without commensurate accuracy gains. Existing training-based approaches to reasoning compression often incur substantial adaptation costs, while inference-time methods are brittle and difficult to scale. These limitations motivate model merging as a promising training-free direction for transferring specialised behaviours between models in a shared parameter space. In particular, merging a slow-thinking model with a fast-thinking counterpart provides a natural mechanism for balancing recommendation accuracy and reasoning conciseness. To this end, we propose, to our knowledge, the first model merging framework for reasoning compression in recommender systems. Unlike conventional merging methods that apply uniform merge coefficients across model components, our method performs fine-grained merging at the level of individual attention heads, capturing heterogeneous patterns in recommendation reasoning. Each attention head is assigned a distinct merge coefficient according to its contribution to critical reasoning evidence and its sensitivity to parameter change, enabling selective injection of the concise behaviour of the fast-thinking model into the slow-thinking model and reducing reasoning verbosity without compromising recommendation quality. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that our method reduces reasoning length by up to 24.3% while outperforming competitive model merging baselines in maintaining recommendation accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linhledieu/REAM.

</details>


### [117] [When Do Anchor-Based Pointwise LLM Rerankers Help? Retriever Quality, Statistical Scope, and Anchor Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10528)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对基于锚点的逐点LLM重排序方法（以GCCP/PAGC为代表）进行了以复现为先的受控研究。作者发现仅凭论文文本复现只能达到0.24 nDCG@10（原报告为0.66），在恢复8个未记录的实现细节后才在1.6%误差内复现结果。研究发现对比式打分核心思想稳健，但两个原论文中固定的设计选择并不可靠：对比分数与逐点相关分数的结合仅在BM25检索器下有效，对更强密集检索器（如E5）几乎无增益；原论文中复杂的锚点构造方法不必要，简单的交错排列句级锚点即可匹配或超越其效果。这些发现跨多种LLM骨干一致，表明锚点式逐点重排序的有效性主要来自对比式打分，且生效条件比原评估所示更窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点式逐点LLM重排序方法声称能以逐点成本恢复跨文档上下文，但对其真实有效性和适用条件缺乏系统研究。作者采用复现优先的方法，先验证可复现性，再进行受控组件级压力测试，以回答"这种方法在什么条件下真正有效"这一核心问题。

Method: 采用复现优先的研究策略：首先仅基于论文文本进行重实现，发现性能差距后逐步恢复8个未记录的实现细节，获得验证过的实现（误差在1.6%以内）；然后利用该实现进行受控组件级分析，分别测试对比式打分思想、分数融合策略（对比分数+标准逐点相关分数）、以及锚点构造方法的影响，并在不同检索器（BM25 vs E5）和不同LLM骨干（含4-bit量化72B模型）下进行跨条件验证。

Result: 核心对比式打分思想在严格统计校正下稳健；但对比分数与标准逐点相关分数结合仅在BM25首阶段检索器下有益，对E5等更强密集模型几乎无增益；原论文的复杂锚点构造方法不必要，简单的交错排列句级锚点即可匹配或超越原方法。这些结论在不同LLM骨干（包括4-bit量化72B模型）上保持一致。

Conclusion: 锚点式逐点重排序方法确实有效，但其增益主要来自对比式打分这一核心思想，而非复杂的聚合和锚点构造选择；且其有效条件比原论文评估所暗示的更窄。该研究强调了复现验证对于判断方法真实贡献的重要性。

Abstract: Anchor-based pointwise LLM reranking scores each candidate against a shared reference passage to recover cross-document context at pointwise cost. We study when this actually helps, using GCCP/PAGC as a representative method. Our study is reproduction-first. We use reproduction as a starting point for a controlled component-level stress test of anchor-based pointwise reranking. Our initial reimplementation, based only on the paper text, achieves 0.24 nDCG@10 instead of the reported 0.66, revealing that several undocumented implementation details are necessary to reproduce the method. After identifying and recovering eight such details, we reproduce the reported results within 1.6% and use the validated implementation for controlled analysis.
  We find that the core contrastive scoring idea is robust under rigorous statistical correction. However, two design choices held fixed in the original paper are less reliable. First, we find that combining the contrastive score with the standard pointwise relevance score helps when the first-stage retriever is BM25, but gives little or no benefit when the first-stage retriever is a stronger dense model such as E5. Second, the paper's more complex method for constructing the anchor is unnecessary. A much simpler anchor, built by interleaving the top-ranked sentences, matches or outperforms it across datasets. These findings are consistent across different LLM backbones, including a 4-bit quantized 72B model. Overall, anchor-based pointwise reranking is effective, but its gains come mainly from contrastive scoring rather than from the more complex aggregation and anchor-construction choices, and they appear under narrower conditions than the original evaluation suggests.

</details>


### [118] [Multi Interests for Joint Search-Recommendation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10535)
*Xiangchen Pan,Wei Wei,Huakang Niu,Zhicong Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIJSR框架，从结构和语义双视角对搜索-推荐混合行为序列进行多兴趣挖掘与自适应融合，提升搜索和推荐的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域统一建模框架虽然能补偿域间行为差异，但忽略了混合序列中不同场景下兴趣表达的差异性，缺乏对用户多兴趣的细粒度建模。

Method: MIJSR包含三个模块：1) 跨域行为融合——通过对比学习对齐查询与物品表示；2) 多兴趣挖掘——结构上通过子序列划分和掩码设置提取搜索兴趣、推荐兴趣和跨域兴趣，语义上利用查询语义信息聚类并对混合序列进行语义分割；3) 多任务预测——将自适应融合的多兴趣与辅助信息结合，使用渐进式分层提取模型进行多任务预测。

Result: 在两个开源数据集上的大量实验表明，MIJSR能够通过细粒度挖掘用户多兴趣进一步提升搜索和推荐的准确性。

Conclusion: 从结构和语义双视角对搜索-推荐混合序列进行多兴趣挖掘是可行且有效的，可为跨域统一建模提供新的思路。

Abstract: Search and recommendation are crucial for understanding user preferences. More and more studies are attempting to jointly model search behavior and recommendation behavior, by integrating user active search and passive recommendation behavior data to better mine user preferences. However, although existing cross-domain unified modeling frameworks can effectively compensate for the differences in behavior between domains, they overlook the expression of interests in different scenarios under mixed sequences. In this study, we propose a multi-interest-based mixed sequential modeling framework MIJSR, which performs multi-interest mining and adaptive integration on search recommendation mixed sequences from both structural and semantic perspectives. Specifically, our model can be roughly divided into three modules: cross-domain behavior fusion, multi-interest mining, and multi-task prediction. Firstly, we align the representations of query and item through contrastive learning training. Then, we extract the multi interests of the mixed behavior sequence from both structural and semantic perspectives. Structurally, we extract search interests, recommendation interests, and cross interests through subsequence partitioning and mask settings; In terms of semantics, we use the semantic information of queries for clustering and perform semantic segmentation on mixed sequences to construct semantic multi interests. Finally, the adaptive fusion of multiple interests is combined with other side information to use a progressive layered extraction model for multi-task prediction. Extensive experiments on two open-source datasets have shown that our model can further enhance its accuracy in search and recommendation by extracting users' multi interests at a fine-grained level. Codes are available at https://github.com/pxcstart/MIJSR.

</details>


### [119] [DistilVDR: A Compact End-to-End Visual Document Retriever via Dual-Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10636)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Ziyi Wang,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DistilVDR，一个524M参数的端到端视觉文档检索系统，从8B视觉语言教师模型中双向蒸馏而来，在ViDoRe基准上达到61.74的平均NDCG@5（教师的86.9%），同时索引体积缩小15.6倍、构建速度快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索（VDR）依赖数十亿参数的大模型，索引速度慢且服务成本高。先前的压缩方法要么从头训练较小的多向量编码器，要么只蒸馏查询侧，均无法端到端地产生紧凑的单向量检索器。

Method: 采用点级余弦对齐损失，从单个8B视觉语言教师模型双侧（查询侧和文档侧）蒸馏一个非对称的编码器-only学生模型。所有监督信号来自冻结教师的嵌入空间（其本身带有相关性监督），因此学生目标无需相关性标签、负采样或对比项。学生模型在文档侧集中视觉能力，查询侧仅70M参数。发布两个变体，共享相同编码器和训练，仅文档编码器的视觉tile预算不同。

Result: DistilVDR-HiRes在ViDoRe v1+v2+v3上达到61.74平均NDCG@5（教师的86.9%），在高分辨率敏感的v3基准上领先所有可复现的sub-1B基线。DistilVDR-Fast以3倍更小的视觉token预算达到59.98。两个变体存储100万文档的索引比最强sub-1B多向量基线小15.6倍，构建速度快一个数量级。

Conclusion: DistilVDR证明了通过双向蒸馏和点级余弦对齐损失，可以实现紧凑的单向量VDR检索器，在保持接近8B教师性能的同时大幅降低存储和索引成本，为VDR的实用化部署提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval (VDR) is dominated by multi-billion-parameter models that are slow to index at full corpus scale and expensive to serve. Prior compression routes either train a smaller multi-vector encoder from scratch or distil only the query side; neither yields a compact single-vector retriever end-to-end. We present DistilVDR, a 524M end-to-end VDR system distilled bilaterally from a single 8B vision-language teacher under a pointwise cosine alignment loss. All supervision comes from the frozen teacher's embedding space, which was itself trained with relevance supervision, so the student objective needs no relevance labels, negative sampling, or contrastive term. We match VDR's text-query and image-document input asymmetry with an asymmetric encoder-only student that concentrates visual capacity on the document side and keeps the query side at 70M parameters. We release two variants that share the same encoders and training and differ only in the document encoder's visual-tile budget: DistilVDR-HiRes attains 61.74 average NDCG@5 on ViDoRe v1+v2+v3 (86.9% of the 8B teacher) and leads every reproduced sub-1B baseline on the high-resolution-sensitive v3 benchmark, while DistilVDR-Fast attains 59.98 at a 3 times smaller visual-token budget. Both variants store one million documents in a 15.6 times smaller index than the strongest sub-1B multi-vector baseline and index the corpus an order of magnitude faster. The code is available at https://github.com/Ryenhails/NanoVDR.

</details>


### [120] [ENTLORE: A Graph-Grounded Benchmark for Latent Organizational Reasoning in Enterprise Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10679)
*Akrin Zheng,Alexander Wu,Alaia Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ENTLORE，一个基于图的企业问答基准构建框架，从日常文档、组织表和运营记录中重建审计过的企业世界。基准包含2,341篇文档和907个问题，覆盖显式查找、跨源组合和潜在组织推理三类任务。实验发现即使提供黄金文档，仍有30.4%的潜在推理问题无法回答，表明企业问答不仅依赖文档召回，还取决于隐式组织关系是否可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业问答基准虽然提供多源证据，但往往预设了答案路径，只能测试既定事实的组成，无法测试从语料库中缺失的目标关系中进行推理的能力（即潜在组织推理）。日常企业记录是工作副产品，隐式的组织关系分散在异构来源中，需要专门的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 提出ENTLORE框架：从日常文档、权威组织表和运营记录中重建经过审计的企业世界；通过版本化的组织约定在真值图中认证派生关系，生成完整的黄金答案和证明证书；对齐的匿名化发布仅暴露文档语料库，隐藏私有结构和目标关系。评估覆盖56种模型和访问配置，支持将发布世界结构化为诱导实体图或可导航知识库。

Result: ENTLORE包含2,341篇来自三种来源类型的文档和907个问题，覆盖显式查找、跨源组合和潜在组织推理。结构化实体图或可导航知识库的部署效果最强。但即便提供黄金文档，潜在推理问题仍有30.4%无法回答，而显式和组合问题分别仅为12.6%和6.2%。

Conclusion: 企业QA不仅取决于文档召回，还取决于隐式组织关系是否变得可用。即使在黄金文档条件下，潜在组织推理问题仍存在显著性能缺口，说明需要进一步研究如何让隐式关系被模型有效利用。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering is framed as retrieving internal documents and generating grounded answers. Routine enterprise records, however, are work by-products in which required organizational relations remain implicit across heterogeneous sources. Existing benchmarks provide realistic multi-source evidence, but often materialize a predefined answer path and therefore test the composition of stated facts rather than recovery of a target relation absent from the corpus. We call the latter capability latent organizational reasoning.
  We introduce ENTLORE, a graph-grounded benchmark construction framework that reconstructs an audited enterprise world from routine documents, authoritative organizational tables, and operational records. Versioned organizational conventions certify derived relations in a truth graph, enabling complete golden answers and proof certificates. The aligned anonymized release exposes only the document corpus while withholding private structure and target relations. ENTLORE contains 2,341 documents from three source types and 907 questions spanning explicit lookup, cross-source composition, and latent organizational reasoning, evaluated across 56 model and access configurations. Structuring the released world as an induced entity graph or navigable knowledge base gives the strongest deployable results. Yet supplying gold documents still leaves 30.4% of latent questions unanswered, versus 12.6% and 6.2% for explicit and compositional questions. Enterprise QA therefore depends not only on document recall, but also on whether implicit organizational relations become usable. The benchmark, data, and code are publicly available at ENTLORE.

</details>


### [121] [Deciding When to Rely on Visual Information: Gated Multimodal Fusion in Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10700)
*Natalija Glisovic,Danica Kragic,Martin Tegner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出VisGate框架，通过自适应门控机制按需融合视觉与协同信号，处理多模态顺序推荐中"视觉信息并非对每个物品或用户都同样有用"的问题。视觉表示通过对比学习从序列共现模式中提取，与协同嵌入保持互补而非对齐。实验表明视觉效用因物品而异，在交互稀疏时更强，且与视觉显著性和语义可解释性相关。门控行为本身可作为一种测量工具，用于理解和解释视觉信息何时有效、为何有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态顺序推荐系统将视觉和协同信号均匀融合，将视觉特征视为对所有物品和用户都同等有用的一般性信息。作者认为视觉效用（视觉信号对推荐质量的贡献）是一个潜在的情境变量，既取决于物品本身，也取决于用户的交互历史，而非固定的物品属性。这一观点挑战了"均匀融合"的假设，需要更细粒度的自适应融合策略。

Method: VisGate框架包含两个核心设计：（1）自适应的物品层级融合决策，门控机制同时基于物品嵌入和用户当前序列上下文来动态决定视觉信号的融合权重；（2）视觉表示通过对比学习目标从序列共现模式中学习，旨在保留与协同嵌入的互补性，而非将两者强制对齐到共享空间。此外，通过学习得到的门控行为可被用作测量工具来分析视觉效用。

Result: VisGate在推荐性能上具有竞争力；分析发现视觉效用随物品不同而变化、在交互稀疏（协同信号弱）时更为显著、并与视觉独特性在语义层面呈现有意义的相关性。这些结果验证了细粒度融合与模态互补性的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究发现表明，多模态顺序推荐需要从"统一均匀融合"转向"情境化自适应融合"。视觉效用可以通过学习到的门控行为进行估计和解释，这为理解多模态信号在推荐中的角色提供了新的分析视角和工具。未来的工作可在更细粒度的用户-物品交互层面进一步探索视觉效用的动态变化。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommender systems commonly fuse visual and collaborative signals uniformly, treating visual features as generically informative regardless of item or user context. We argue that visual utility, defined as the contribution of visual signals to recommendation quality, is a latent contextual variable that depends on both the item and the user's interaction history rather than a fixed item property. To model this variability, we introduce VisGate, a framework that makes adaptive item-level fusion decisions conditioned on item embeddings and the user's current sequence context. Visual representations are learned through a contrastive objective over sequential co-occurrence patterns, preserving complementarity with collaborative embeddings rather than aligning them into a shared space. Beyond achieving competitive recommendation performance, VisGate's learned gate serves as a measurement tool for understanding when and why visual information is beneficial. Our analyses show that visual utility varies across items, increases under interaction sparsity when collaborative signals are weak, and correlates with visual distinctiveness in semantically meaningful ways. Together, these findings highlight the importance of both fine-grained fusion and modality complementarity, while demonstrating that item-level visual utility can be estimated and interpreted through learned gating behaviour.

</details>


### [122] [TimeRoute: Time-Aware Modality Routing and Diffusion for Multi-Modal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10983)
*Pengyu Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Klim Zaporojets,Congfeng Cao,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeRoute，一个基于扩散的统一多模态推荐框架，通过时间感知的模态路由和双流去噪的扩散图重构器，解决多模态信号在不同时间尺度上漂移导致的模态时间尺度不匹配问题。在TikTok、Amazon-Baby和Amazon-Sports上，Recall@K、Precision@K和NDCG@K最多提升9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐器中不同模态（文本、图像、音频）的有用性随时间漂移且速率不同，导致两个耦合挑战：(1)用户在不同时间上下文需要不同的模态比例；(2)不太相关的模态更容易引入过时或误导性信号。现有工作使用全局共享的融合权重，无法适应这种时间尺度不匹配。

Method: TimeRoute包含两部分：一是时间感知模态路由器，将用户聚合行为特征映射到个性化模态分布，替代全局共享融合权重；二是扩散驱动的图重构器，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）以同一时间特征为条件，配以双流长短时去噪头，在模态边进入传播图前抑制过时信号。

Result: 在TikTok、Amazon-Baby和Amazon-Sports三个数据集上，经过10个随机种子的配对检验，Recall@K、Precision@K和NDCG@K相对强基线一致提升，最高达9.8%。

Conclusion: TimeRoute通过将时间信息统一注入模态路由和扩散去噪过程，有效解决了多模态推荐中模态时间尺度不匹配问题，验证了个性化、时间感知的模态融合比全局固定权重更优。

Abstract: Multi-modal recommenders fuse collaborative signals with item modalities such as text, images, and audio, but the usefulness of each drifts over time and at different rates. For example, chocolate purchases typically guided by textual ingredient cues can shift toward visual packaging and ambient audio around Valentine's Day. This modality time-scale mismatch gives rise to two coupled challenges: (1) users require different modality proportions across temporal contexts, and (2) less relevant modalities are more likely to introduce outdated or misleading signals into the recommender. We address both challenges within a unified diffusion-based recommender, TimeRoute. A temporal-aware modal router maps each user's aggregated behavioral features to a personalized modality distribution, replacing the globally shared fusion weights used in prior work. The diffusion-based graph reconstructor is then conditioned on the same temporal profile through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) with dual-stream long- and short-term denoising heads, suppressing outdated modality edges before they enter the propagation graph. Experiments on TikTok, Amazon-Baby, and Amazon-Sports demonstrate consistent improvements of up to 9.8\% in Recall@K, Precision@K, and NDCG@K over strong baselines across 10-seed paired tests. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TimeRoute.

</details>


### [123] [Sona Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11015)
*Sona Team,Alexandr Udeneev,Aleksei Krasilnikov,Alexey Nadtochiy,Andrey Semenov,Andrey Tsyrkunov,Anna Krivonos,Anna Lipkina,Artem Matveev,Daniil Burlakov,Daniil Leschev,Daria Tikhonovich,Denis Burshtein,Ekaterina Dmitrieva,Eugene Krofto,Grigorii Khlystov,Ilya Murzin,Kirill Golovko,Ksenia Sycheva,Leonid Dmitriev,Mariia Rozaeva,Mariia Ulianova,Mikhail Sandul,Nikolai Savushkin,Oleg Sorokin,Roman Odobesku,Semyon Panenko,Sergei Liamaev,Sergei Makeev,Vadim Shilov,Veronika Ivanova,Viktor Yanush,Vladimir Baikalov,Vladislav Dodonov,Vladislav Tytskiy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Sona，一个用于Yandex Music的单一模型生成式推荐系统。在在线A/B测试中，Sona用单个联合训练的模型替代了包含15+候选生成器及多阶段排序模型的完整生产级联架构，同时在活跃用户数、总收听时长和点赞数等关键指标上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的推荐系统采用多阶段级联架构（候选生成+预排序+精排序），涉及大量手工特征和多个模型的协同，系统复杂且维护成本高。作者希望探索是否可以用一个单一的统一模型替代整个生产级联，同时保持甚至提升推荐质量。

Method: Sona架构将候选生成和排序统一在共享的用户表示周围。编码器将用户的时序行为序列转化为隐藏状态，同时供自回归解码器和排序模块使用。通过next-token-prediction和蒸馏目标联合更新编码器，将生成和排序耦合在同一用户状态中。训练时使用更大的Teacher Ranker提供排序目标，但服务时仅部署单一模型（编码器+解码器+排序模块），不使用任何手工特征。

Result: 在Yandex Music最大的推荐界面之一"My Vibe"智能音箱上进行的在线A/B实验中，Sona相比生产对照组在主要指标活跃用户数上提升4.53%（统计显著），总收听时长提升6.30%，点赞数提升11.42%。活跃用户数提升幅度是该界面此前最强模型Argus部署增量的2.35倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，一个单一联合训练的模型可以替代成熟的多阶段推荐级联，并在实时流量上提升推荐质量，证明了统一模型架构在工业级推荐系统中的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Sona, a single-model generative recommender for Yandex Music. In an online A/B test, Sona replaced the entire production cascade, comprising more than 15 candidate generators followed by pre-ranking and ranking models that consume hundreds of features, including signals from large transformer models such as Argus and target-attention scorers, while significantly improving key engagement metrics.
  The architecture of Sona unifies candidate generation and ranking around a shared user representation. Its encoder transforms the user's chronological sequence of logged engagement events into hidden states consumed by both the autoregressive decoder and the Ranking Module. The next-token-prediction and distillation objectives jointly update the encoder, coupling generation and ranking through the same user state. Neither Sona nor its Teacher Ranker uses hand-engineered features; both operate on logged event fields and learned item representations. In the final Sona configuration, the larger teacher supplies ranking targets during training but is absent from serving, leaving the encoder, decoder, and Ranking Module as a single deployed model.
  We evaluate Sona in an online A/B experiment using live traffic from My Vibe on smart speakers, one of Yandex Music's largest recommendation surfaces. Relative to the production control, Sona produced statistically significant uplifts of 4.53% in Active Users, the primary metric, 6.30% in Total Listening Time, and 11.42% in Likes. These effects were incremental to improvements retained from preceding deployments. The Active Users uplift was 2.35 times the increment previously delivered by Argus, the strongest model deployed on this surface before Sona. These results show that a single jointly trained model can replace a mature multi-stage recommendation cascade while improving recommendation quality on live traffic.

</details>


### [124] [Self-Knowledge Retrieval Augmented Generation Framework for Patent Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11030)
*Jian Zhang,Songlin Lei,Zhuohao Yang,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自知识RAG框架的专利检索与匹配方法，引导大语言模型自动提取关键技术实体并构建层级本体结构，结合FAISS检索与生成式匹配机制，在真实专利数据集上显著提升了检索和匹配的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利文档结构复杂、技术术语密集且包含多模态信息，传统方法难以准确识别专利间的细微差异；现有基于LLM的专利匹配方法依赖领域预训练或指令微调，存在高人工标注成本和灾难性遗忘问题，而传统RAG方法未能充分利用LLM自动解析专利和挖掘深层语义关系的能力。

Method: 提出自知识RAG框架，引导LLM从专利匹配查询中自主提取关键技术实体并构建层级本体结构，实现查询扩展和精确检索；集成FAISS向量检索与生成式匹配机制，利用自知识增强模型对专利创新的理解。

Result: 在真实专利数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法在专利检索和匹配任务上表现出优异的性能，显著优于现有方法，验证了其有效性和应用潜力。

Conclusion: 自知识RAG框架能够有效解决专利匹配中的细微语义识别难题，无需昂贵的人工标注和领域微调即可提升LLM的专利理解与检索匹配能力，具有广阔的知识产权保护应用前景。

Abstract: Patent retrieval and matching based on large language models (LLMs) play a vital role in intellectual property protection. However, due to the complex structure of patent documents, dense technical terminology, and multi-modal information, traditional methods struggle to accurately identify subtle differences between patents. Existing LLM-based patent matching approaches typically rely on domain-specific pretrained or instruction tuning, which often entail high manual labeling costs and catastrophic forgetting. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods introduce external knowledge they fail to fully leverage LLM's capability to automatically parse patents and mine deep semantic relationships. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a self-knowledge RAG framework that guides LLMs to autonomously extract key technical entities and construct hierarchical ontological structures from patent matching queries, thereby enabling query expansion and precise retrieval. The method integrates the FAISS retrieval with a generative matching mechanism, leveraging self-knowledge to enhance the model's understanding of patent innovations and significantly improve retrieval and matching accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate the outstanding performance of the proposed method on real-world patent datasets, validating its effectiveness and application potential.

</details>


### [125] [Role of Personality in Conversational Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11164)
*Abdisalam Abukar,Junchen Fu,Chengli Zhai,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究对话式信息检索中助手个性（外向、尽责、中性）对用户行为与交互质量的影响，发现助手个性是一种情境敏感的设计变量，而非可全局优化的系统属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被用于信息检索场景，但人们对用户个性、助手个性与任务情境三者的联合影响知之甚少。

Method: 采用受控被试内设计，26名参与者分别完成探索性旅行规划、对比性智能手机购物和健康饮食信息检索三类任务，并在三种助手个性条件（外向、尽责、中性）下交互，采集对话日志、行为轨迹、问卷及大五人格测量数据。

Result: 外向助手产生更长的对话轮次，尽责助手引发更高的用户词占比和更多轮次，中性基线介于两者之间；最强的效应是任务与助手个性的交互作用对信任和委托决策的影响，不同任务偏好不同个性风格，无全局最优个性。

Conclusion: 助手个性应被视为情境敏感的交互设计变量，参与者强烈偏好个性可选或可自适应调整的交互方式，而非固定不变的系统属性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for information seeking, where users find, compare, and evaluate information through dialogue. In this role, the assistant does more than retrieve or generate content: it shapes how users articulate constraints, ask follow-up questions, verify claims, and decide when an answer is sufficient for action. Yet little is known about how user personality, assistant personality, and task context jointly influence these interactions. We examine personality as a controllable variable in conversational information seeking and study its effects on user behaviour and interaction quality. We conducted a controlled within-subject study in which assistant personality and task type were experimentally varied, while participant personality was measured using Big Five scores. Twenty-six participants each completed three information-seeking tasks under three assistant personality conditions: extraverted, conscientious, and neutral. Tasks covered exploratory travel planning, comparative smartphone shopping, and verification-sensitive health and diet information seeking. Data included conversation logs, behavioural traces, post-interaction questionnaires, an exit questionnaire, and Big Five measures. The assistant conditions were behaviourally distinct: the extraverted assistant produced longer turns, the conscientious assistant elicited higher user word share and more turns, and the neutral baseline fell between them. The strongest effect was a task-by-assistant interaction on trust and delegation, with preferred styles varying by task. No global winner emerged, but participants strongly preferred style choice or adaptation. These findings position assistant personality as a context-sensitive interactional design variable rather than a globally optimisable system property.

</details>


### [126] [Are We Really Making Progress in Group Recommendation? Unmasking the Tie-Breaking Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11190)
*Song-Duo Ma,Pu-Jen Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了一个系统性的评估偏差：多个近期组推荐方法在训练时使用sigmoid压缩分数，导致评估时产生大量并列最高分数，进而使HR@K和NDCG@K等top-K指标对平局处理方式高度敏感。作者提出平局感知评估协议（精确计算均匀随机平局下的指标期望），发现许多以往报告的改进在平局感知评估下大幅缩水，方法排名也发生显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近组推荐方法在标准基准上取得了显著改进，但这些改进是否真正反映了模型组偏好建模能力的实质性提升尚不明确。作者怀疑存在系统性的评估偏差，可能掩盖了这些方法的真实性能。

Method: 1) 识别训练时sigmoid压缩与评估时确定性平局处理的交互作用导致的评估偏差；2) 在CAMRa2011和Mafengwo数据集上，对近期代表性方法和基线在组推荐与用户推荐两种设置下进行重评估；3) 提出平局感知评估协议，精确计算均匀随机平局下HR@K和NDCG@K的期望值；4) 分析额外sigmoid在优化中的隐式边际平滑作用，并验证温度缩放BPR的替代方案。

Result: 许多先前报告的改进在平局感知评估下显著缩水，方法的相对排名发生明显变化。额外sigmoid可能充当隐式边际平滑器，而温度缩放BPR在保留大部分收益的同时避免严重的平局膨胀问题。

Conclusion: 平局感知评估对于在组推荐领域建立可靠的研究进展至关重要，研究者应关注评估协议的严谨性，以避免虚假的性能改进。

Abstract: Recent group recommendation methods have reported strong improvements on standard benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains always reflect genuine advances in modeling group preferences. In this paper, we show that several recent methods are affected by a systematic evaluation bias caused by the interaction between training-time score compression and evaluation-time deterministic tie-breaking. Specifically, an additional sigmoid transformation before the BPR objective can greatly increase tied top scores, making top-K metrics such as HR@K and NDCG@K highly sensitive to how ties are resolved. We revisit recent representative methods and their baselines on CAMRa2011 and Mafengwo under both group and user recommendation settings, and evaluate them with a tie-aware protocol that computes the exact expectation of HR@K and NDCG@K under uniform random tie-breaking. Our results show that many previously reported improvements shrink substantially under tie-aware evaluation, and the relative ranking of methods can change markedly. We further show that the additional sigmoid may act as implicit margin smoothing during optimization, and that temperature-scaled BPR can retain much of this benefit without inducing severe tie inflation. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of tie-aware evaluation for establishing reliable progress in group recommendation. The code is available at https://github.com/songduoma/TieAwareGroupRec.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [AI Query Compilation for Unified and Optimized Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10139)
*Yeounoh Chung,Helena Caminal,Fatma Ozcan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向加速AI查询执行的统一编译执行架构，将SQL关系运算与LLM推理层整合为单一张量计算图，消除PCIe数据搬运瓶颈，在TPU上实现最高5.3倍延迟加速和9.8倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI查询执行在SQL关系运算与LLM推理之间存在跨执行边界的PCIe数据搬运瓶颈，严重限制了查询执行的性能，需要一种新的统一执行范式来消除该瓶颈。

Method: 提出将混合AI查询整体编译为一个统一的张量计算图，同时整合标准SQL关系运算与LLM推理层，从而支持全局编译器优化和高效的自动分片（automatic sharding）。

Result: 在SemBench Reviews和Movies数据集上的select及扩展AI查询实验中，在TPU上实现了最高5.3倍延迟加速和9.8倍吞吐量提升，验证了统一执行范式的可行性。

Conclusion: 统一编译执行架构在加速AI查询方面展现出显著潜力，未来还需探索一系列开放的技术挑战以完善和实现这一愿景。

Abstract: In this vision paper, we propose a novel architectural paradigm for accelerated AI query execution via a unified compiled execution strategy. By compiling the hybrid AI Query as a whole -- integrating both standard SQL relational constructs and LLM inference layers into a single, unified tensor compute graph -- we completely alleviate PCIe data movement bottlenecks across execution boundaries and enable global compiler optimizations and efficient automatic sharding. We demonstrate the viability of this unified execution paradigm on select and extended AI queries on SemBench Reviews and Movies datasets, achieving up to 5.3x latency speedup and 9.8x throughput speedup on TPUs, and outline a research roadmap of open technical challenges to realize this vision.

</details>


### [128] [Time to Move on: Querying without Nulls and Bags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10863)
*Molham Aref,Leonid Libkin,Wim Martens*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文基于设计关系编程语言Rel的实践经验，论证了完全规范化关系（无bag、无null）的查询语言是可行且更优越的，并逐一反驳了SQL世界中为bags和nulls辩护的常见理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL因声明性而成功，但随着问题复杂度提升，早期语言设计决策（如允许bags和nulls）需要重新审视。作者认为这些被视为"不可避免之恶"的设计缺陷实际上完全可以避免。

Method: 作者通过Rel语言的设计与部署实践，基于完全规范化关系（set语义、无null）构建端到端关系编程语言，并对支持bags和nulls的常见论据进行逐一驳斥分析。

Result: 作者成功设计、实现并部署了基于完全规范化关系的Rel语言，证明去除bags和nulls是可行的，并揭示了支持这些特性的论据在仔细考察下均不成立。

Conclusion: bags和nulls并非不可避免的邪恶，无null、set语义的关系语言不仅可行，还提供了诸多新机遇，值得数据库社区重新思考早期语言设计决策。

Abstract: SQL is the database community's success story in terms of language design. The key reason for its success is its declarativeness: it gives rise to optimizability, reducing the programmer's burden significantly. However, given the evolving complexity of problems to solve with query languages, our community needs to re-think some of the fundamental early query language design decisions. Our experience in having worked on the design of Rel (a language for end-to-end relational programming) tells us that it is possible to design, implement, and successfully deploy a language based on fully normalized relations. Such relations avoid what Codd called corrupted relations and what we commonly refer to as bags, and the 'harmful' 'billion dollar mistake' that we know as nulls. In the SQL world, it is accepted that bags and nulls are tolerated as an unavoidable evil. We argue that the evil is completely avoidable: reasons offered for justifying bags and nulls evaporate at a closer examination. In addition to debunking them, we also describe opportunities offered by a null-free language with set semantics.

</details>


### [129] [Property Graph Techniques in Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11001)
*Malcolm Crowe,Fritz Laux*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何通过将SQL外键建模为引用键，在单一关系数据库上统一实现关系型数据库和属性图数据库（PGDBMS）的功能，支持图插入和高级模式匹配，并允许多种类型图模型的构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据库管理系统标准化活动中，SQL新版本和标签属性图标准正在并行发展，但关系型数据库和属性图数据库的实现机制存在割裂，需要探索统一实现的途径。

Method: 将SQL外键（foreign keys）建模为引用键（reference keys），以此作为桥梁来统一记录型关系数据库和属性图数据库的实现，支持图插入操作以及PGDBMS的先进模式匹配机制。

Result: 通过引用键建模方法，能够在单一关系数据库上构建多种类型的图模型，实现关系数据库与属性图数据库功能的统一集成。

Conclusion: 将SQL外键建模为引用键是实现关系型与属性图数据库统一实现的有效机制，为标准化活动中的SQL与属性图标准融合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Current developments in standardization activities for database management systems include the development of the next versions of the Structured Query Language (SQL), and related standards for labelled property graphs. This short paper explores mechanisms for unified implementation of record-based relational and property graph databases: its contribution is that modeling SQL foreign keys as reference keys facilitates the graph insert and advanced pattern matching mechanisms of Property Graph Database Management Systems (PGDMS) and allows the construction of multiple type graph models on a single relational database.

</details>
